Shams al-Maʿārif al-Kubrā — Verbatim English Edition

This translation is a verbatim, literal rendering of the original Arabic. No interpretation, commentary, or cultural adaptation has been added. Technical terms, honorifics, invocations, and ambiguous constructions are preserved as-is. When forced to choose between smooth English and accurate English, accuracy is chosen.

Source text: Cairo edition, collated against Egypt & India editions plus the al-Ḥajj Mirzā Ḥusayn manuscript. OCR & enrichment via Gemini 2.0 Flash.

This is a work-in-progress partial edition (pages 1–119 of 604).

— Preface —
Preface — The First Part
الجزء الأول من كتاب شمس المعارف الكبرى ولطائف العوارف
The first part of the book Shams al-Maʿārif al-Kubrā wa-Laṭāʾif al-ʿAwārif

[p. 7]

The first part from the book Shams al-Ma‘arif al-Kubra and Lata’if al-‘Awarif by the Shaykh Ahmad ibn Ali al-Buni, may Allah sanctify his spirit, deceased in the year 622, twenty-two and six hundred He said in Kashf al-Zunun: and the intended purpose of this book is that by it one may know the nobility of the Names of Allah تعالى and what has been deposited in their sea of kinds of the wisdom-jewels, and how the disposition by the names and the invocations, and what follows them of the letters of the surahs and the verses, so that by them one may connect to the Divine Presence without toil and connect by them to the desires of the world without trouble. انتهى

❖ And following it ❖

Risalat Mizan al-‘Adl fi Maqasid Ahkam al-Raml ❖ and Risalat Fawatih al-Ragha’ib fi Khususat Awtat al-Kawakib ❖ and Risalat Zahr al-Muruj fi Dala’il al-Buruj ❖ and Risalat Lata’if al-Isharah fi Khasa’is al-Kawakib al-Sayyarah ❖ authored by the learned scholar, the noble السيد عبد القادر الحسيني الأدهمي

❖ May Allah benefit the Muslims by his sciences, amen

❖ Notice ❖

Since the book Shams al-Ma‘arif, because of the شدت need of people for it, has been printed many times (in Egypt and India), and the Egyptian presses have taken care in correcting it, yet it has not been free from error and distortion due to the absence of correct copies, and it has been printed this time collated against copies of “Egypt and India” and another copy in the handwriting of Hajj Mirza Husayn, with precision in correction by the knowledge of a committee from the eminent scholars of Egypt, and it was corrected again by the great professor Shaykh Abd al-Rahman al-Jaziri, so this copy has come, praise be to Allah, as the most excellent of copies in printing and correction Printed at the press of Mustafa al-Babi al-Halabi and his sons in Egypt

[p. 8]

And to Allah belong the Most Beautiful Names, so call upon Him by them.

In the name of Allah, the Merciful, the Compassionate.

A testimony, pre-eternity; and from the light of this testimony the chosen ones drew up knowledge, so understand that, and the arrangement which appeared in the two testimonies connected with the noble angels and those possessed of knowledge. So this is the testimony of eternity, for in them is the secret of these two testimonies, the witness of the two kingdoms, and what He deposited in it by the secret of connection with the unveilings, and for every form its reaching to all the wisdoms.

And I ask Allah, the Living, the Self-Subsisting, that He make it a pure, accepted alms before my whispered prayer, and that the spirit of its ease accompany me in my turning about and my abode, and that He make clear for me and for you the path, and bestow upon us and upon you the lights of verification. Verily this lightning-flash, the merciful, and the shining, illuminating suns, are the paths of the knowers, and the way of the veracious, and the broad road of the righteous, for the presence of the holiness of the Lord of the worlds, the Lord of the lords, and the splitter of the causes, and the lifter of the veil, invented without example, the transcendent above forms, the everlasting, who has not ceased to be described with the attributes of beauty, ever-existent in pre-eternity, the lifter of the higher things by His determination and His wisdom, and the spreader of the lower things by His power and His will; there is no god except Him, the Great, the Most High, the veiled by the veils of lights, hidden from all the secrets, concealed from the pavilions of the sights, and He perceives the sights by His inwardness, by His Essence, in His pre-eternity, and He appeared by His attributes in His eternity, and He became manifest by His instruments in His sempiternity, and He manifested Himself by His acts in His eternity. He is the First in pre-eternity, and the Last in eternity, and the Manifest in sempiternity. Exalted is He above substances and accidents, and above bodies and parts, and above being disposed of by purposes. The directions and the regions do not contain Him, and the succession of the cycles does not follow Him, and the passing of night and day does not annihilate Him. I praise Him, glorified is He and exalted, and everything with Him is by measure, Knower of the unseen and the witnessed, the Great, the Most High. And I bear witness that there is no god except Allah, alone, no partner has He, a testimony that accompanies the spirits with تثبيت in the barzakh-realms. And I bear witness for you: His creation has counted it, the living and the dead, and He has seen the dead and the powers, the world with what has passed and what is coming, and the Reviver of the dead after they had been bones. And I bear witness that Muhammad is His servant and His Messenger, the sun of the creed and the deliverer of the servants from shirk and abasement.

[p. 9]

And the one who, by his call, made the sphere of tawhid revolve ❊ and the suns of his wisdom shone forth ❊ and the stars of misguidance sank away by his sight ❊ and the dawn of tawhid broke forth by his felicity ❊ may the best of prayers be upon him and upon his family ❊ and may Allah be pleased with the verifying, truthful Companions ❊ may Allah be pleased with them all together ❊ a prayer that reaches them the highest ranks and the loftiest degrees ❊ (Now then) ❊ then the truth has banners and for the reality there is a system and for the spirits, by the divine knowledges, an acquaintance, and the means is sought and the ability to its divisions is bestowed, and felicity is coupled with the suns of perfection, and eternal experience by the use of the rites of the Shari‘ah is bestowed, and the highest degrees in ‘Illiyyin are the degree of the doers, and the highest of them is the station of the guiding, verifying ones, and there is no station for a scholar in the religion of Allah who does not benefit, just as there is no existence of life for the reality of a soul that does not benefit, and the farthest of people from felicity is he who makes light of the rulings of the millah and breaches the conditions of the verifying ones from among the people of the qiblah (And I), when I saw the speech of the eminent ones, those whose word has become lofty and whose wisdom has spread in the horizons and whose blessing has encompassed the creatures, they had authored in the conjugation of names and attributes and the secrets of letters and remembrances and supplications, and there had inclined to me from one attached to me his affection in clarifying what they had authored and the treasure of what they had stored up, so I answered him, with acknowledgment of incapacity to understand the perceptions of the past forefathers and the guiding verifying imams, and I hoped from Allah, by the exertion of acknowledgment and acquisition, that He would extend to me from the spirits of their spirits with subtle aids, so that the utterance would be in accordance with verification and detailed with the tongue of confirmation (so I say) ❊ in Allah is the sought help and upon Him is the reliance: the intended purpose of the chapters of this book is knowledge of the nobility of the names of Allah تعالى and what Allah تعالى has deposited in their sea of the kinds of الحكميات jewels and divine لطائف, and how to act with the names of the supplications and what followed them of the letters of the surahs and the verses, and I made this book chapters so that every chapter would indicate what I selected and counted of subtle sciences by which one reaches the divine presence without fatigue and without perceiving hardship, and what by them one reaches the desires of the world and what is desired in it, and I named this selected, peerless, lofty-knowledge book (Shams al-Ma‘arif wa Lata’if al-‘Awarif) because of what is within it of subtle acts of disposition and the knowledges of influences. So it is forbidden upon whoever this book of mine falls into his hand that he display it to other than its people or divulge it in other than its place, for whenever he does that Allah تعالى will deprive him of its benefits and its profits and its blessing will be withheld from him, and do not touch it except while you are pure, and do not read it except if you are remembering, so that you may obtain from it what you want, and do not direct it except in what Allah تعالى is pleased with, for it is the book of the awliya’, and the righteous, and the obedient, and the seekers, and the doers, the desirous ones, so be stingy with it and do not leave from it a little nor a lot, and let your certainty be truthful and your faith in its realities confident. Actions are only by intentions, and only for every man is what he intended, and if an intention arises for you in an act among the acts, then believe in it and affirm, according to His saying عليه الصلاة والسلام: let none of you supplicate except while he is certain of the response, and decide upon your act with correctness, according to His saying عليه الصلاة والسلام: if one of you asks, then let him resolve the request, for it is not disliked, and the certainty of response, and decide upon your act with correctness, according to His saying صلى الله عليه وسلم: one of you is answered if he does not hasten, saying, “I supplicated, but I was not answered.” ❊ And beware that you do not deem the response slow, and do not cease being purified always and awaiting it ❊ And this upright law and straight path comprises forty chapters; every chapter comprises meanings and indications and hidden and manifest symbols, so ponder it with your intellect and contemplate it with your thought ❊ And these chapters (the first chapter) are in the letters

[p. 10]

The muʿjama and what is arranged in it of the secrets and the concealments. ﴿ Chapter Two ﴾ in breaking and expansion and the arrangement of works in the times and hours. ﴿ Chapter Three ﴾ in the rulings of the twenty-eight mansions of the moon, the celestial. ﴿ Chapter Four ﴾ in the rulings of the twelve signs of the zodiac and what there is for them of the indications and the connections.

﴿ Chapter Five ﴾ in the secrets of the basmala and what there is for it of the hidden properties and blessings. ﴿ Chapter Six ﴾ in seclusion and the masters of retreat leading to the upper realms. ﴿ Chapter Seven ﴾ in the names by which Jesus upon him السلام used to bring the dead to life. ﴿ Chapter Eight ﴾ in the four tawqīfāt and what there is for them of the chapters and the circles. ﴿ Chapter Nine ﴾ in the properties of the beginnings of the Qur’an and the clear verses. ﴿ Chapter Ten ﴾ in the secrets of al-Fātiḥa and its supplications and its famous properties. ﴿ Chapter Eleven ﴾ in the inventions and in the merciful lights. ﴿ Chapter Twelve ﴾ in the Greatest Name of Allah and what there is for it of the hidden dispositions.

﴿ Chapter Thirteen ﴾ in the fallen parts of al-Fātiḥa and what there is for them of the wafqs and the supplications. ﴿ Chapter Fourteen ﴾ in the spiritual exercises and the remembrances and the answered supplications, the subjugated ones. ﴿ Chapter Fifteen ﴾ in the conditions necessary for some, not for some, in the beginnings to the suns of the endings. ﴿ Chapter Sixteen ﴾ in the Beautiful Names of Allah and their times, the beneficial tried ones. ﴿ Chapter Seventeen ﴾ in the properties of Kهيعص and its letters, the lordly, the most holy. ﴿ Chapter Eighteen ﴾ in the properties of the Verse of the Throne and what is in it of the hidden blessings. ﴿ Chapter Nineteen ﴾ in the properties of some wafqs and the beneficial talismans. ﴿ Chapter Twenty ﴾ in Sūrat Yā Sīn and what there is for it of the answered supplications.

﴿ Chapter Twenty-One ﴾ in the Beautiful Names of Allah and their patterns and what there is for every pattern of the supplications and the dispositions.

﴿ Chapter Twenty-Two ﴾ in the second pattern and what is in it of the gift-names. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Three ﴾ in the third pattern and what indicates the adyāt attributes. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Four ﴾ in the fourth pattern and what is in it of the secrets of the Lord of the creatures. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Five ﴾ in the fifth pattern and what is in it of the selected properties. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Six ﴾ in the sixth pattern and what is in it of the secrets of the necessitating فرضيات.

﴿ Chapter Twenty-Seven ﴾ in the seventh pattern from the Names of Allah تعالى and what there is for them of the blessings. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Eight ﴾ in the eighth pattern from the Beautiful Names and their hidden secrets. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Nine ﴾ in the ninth pattern from the Beautiful Names and what there is for them of the dispositions. ﴿ Chapter Thirty ﴾ in the tenth pattern from the Beautiful Names and their beneficial secrets. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-One ﴾ in the letters of Arabic and what there is for them of the planets and the servants and the minerals and the seclusions. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Two ﴾ in the secrets of uncovering the معنويات of ʿarūḍ. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Three ﴾ in the explanation of the secrets of the Circle of Encompassment and what has appeared from it of the foundations and the definitions. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Four ﴾ in the science of the Zāyirja and the proportions of the letters and the signs of the zodiac and the famous balances. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Five ﴾ in the lettered hidden thing by the Jafr rules. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Six ﴾ in the divine effusion and the radiant light and the honored stone and the properties of plants.

﴿ Chapter Thirty-Seven ﴾ in the works of Sīmiyāʾ and all the sayings. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Eight ﴾ in uses

— Chapter One —
Chapter One — On the Dotted Letters
الفصل الأول في الحروف المعجمة وما فيها من الأسرار والإضمارات
On the dotted letters and what is in them of secrets and hidden meanings

[p. 11]

Uses of the letters and their retreats upon the sentences and the details (the thirty-ninth chapter) in the explanation of the Beautiful Names of God as they came by way of elucidation and details (the fortieth chapter) in the single supplications by which one is supplicated in all the nights and times

The first chapter concerning the dotted letters and what is in them of secrets and hidden meanings

I say, and with God is success and guidance, the aims of the seekers have been divided into two parts, worldly and otherworldly, and each one of them is divided into sections according to the purposes. And people have spoken concerning the opposition of the times and the standing upon the stars and the exercises and the acts of talismans before the setting down of this book and the discourse upon it. And this science is expansive; many creatures have desired it, and upon it especially whoever found for that a great effect. So I desired to oppose that by a description that proceeds in the manner of the sense in the aspects of the people of this science. And the early sages spoke concerning it, and many people agreed to that saying. So those, if they are many in the world, harm in the Hereafter. And this which I mention to you, you benefit by it in the world and the Hereafter.

Chapter concerning the mention of the dotted letters: are they the roots of speech and its foundation, and by them its building is raised

(And know) that for numbers there are secrets just as for letters there are effects, and that the upper world extends to the lower world; so the world of the Throne extends to the world of the Kursi, and the world of the Kursi extends to the sphere of Saturn, and the sphere of Saturn extends to the sphere of Jupiter, and the sphere of Jupiter extends to the sphere of Mars, and the sphere of Mars extends to the sphere of the Sun, and the sphere of the Sun extends to the sphere of Venus, and the sphere of Venus extends to the sphere of Mercury, and the sphere of Mercury extends to the sphere of the Moon, and the sphere of the Moon extends to the sphere of heat, and the sphere of heat extends to the sphere of moisture, and the sphere of moisture extends to the sphere of cold, and the sphere of cold extends to the sphere of dryness, and the sphere of dryness extends to the sphere of air, and the sphere of air extends to the sphere of water, and the sphere of water extends to the sphere of earth, and the sphere of earth extends to the sphere of Saturn. In the upper things is the letter jim, and the numbers falling upon it are three in general; and as for in detail, then fifty-three, thus the mim with four and the ba with three. And it also is with three letters, and for it among the lower things is the letter sad, and it is in number ninety, and those in the upper things in general are five, and it is the letter ha. And for it among the fivefold accords and the sphere of Jupiter is six, and it is the letter waw. And for it among the sixfold accords (And as for) the inflection of the sphere of Venus, for it is the letter zay, and for it among the sevenfold accords. And as for the inflection of the sphere of Mercury, for it is among the number eight, and it is the letter ha, and for it among the eightfold accords. (And as for) the inflection of the sphere of the Moon, for it is among the number nine, and it is the letter ta, and for it among the ninefold accords. And Saturn has the triangle famous among the scholars, so understand that.

Chapter concerning the relation of the human essence

So the Throne has the letter alif, and the Kursi has the letter ba, and Saturn has the letter jim, and thus to the Moon as has preceded.

Chapter) And the letters are of kinds: among them is that by which one begins on the right, and they are the letters of the Arabs; and among them is that by which one begins

on the left, and they are the Roman, the Greek, and the Coptic. And every writing on the right is connected, and every writing on the left is separate, so understand that. (And the letters) are twenty-eight, other than lam-alif, and it is the completion of the twenty-nine, and that is the number of the lunar mansions. And when the mansions, from which fourteen appear above the earth, were such, these letters among them are what is assimilated with the lam of the definition, and they are fourteen letters: t th d dh r z s sh s d t z l n

[p. 12]

And among them is what appears with it, and they are fourteen letters as you see: أ ب ج ح ك م ع غ ف ق هـ و ي. And the first of the letters, alif, and what comes after it of the letters, like the طاعات and the تعريفات and the راءات, and they are from the side of alif and what comes after it. So if a beholder looks at the letters, he finds them an accord in the soul before their existence in the form, so understand. For alif among the letters is the one among the numbers, and the numbers are a subtle spiritual power. So reliance upon it is that from the secrets of sayings, just as the letters are from the secrets of actions. And for the numbers in the world of mankind are secrets and benefits, He, glorious is His power, has arranged them as an arrangement in the letters; secrets of benefit, like supplication and ruqyah and other than that, what its effect appears to the world by kinds of names. (And know) that the letters have no time that confines them; rather, they act by discipline and property for whom He wills, and the numbers act by talismans, so they are connected to the higher considerations. (So the letter dal) has from the numbers four; so whoever establishes a Shakl, let him multiply 4 by 4 and place in it the proportion of Monday, the day the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم was born, and the day of his mission, and the day of his death, in the exaltation of the moon on three degrees of Taurus, safe from the inauspiciousnesses; and let the hour be for the moon, and write it after complete purity and a prayer of two rak‘ahs with Ayat al-Kursi and al-Ikhlas one hundred times on pure parchment. So whoever carries this Wafq with him, Allah تعالى makes easy for him memorization and understanding, and his rank is عظيماً with the upper and lower world altogether. And if a prisoner carries it, he is released, by the permission of Allah تعالى. And whoever carries it on a banner, he defeats the enemies from the disbelievers and the transgressors. And whoever carries it and disputes with someone by means of it, then he overcomes him and subdues him. And its numbers falling upon it are 4 by 4, and its form is from the multiplication of 4 by 4, and it is for the four elements: fire, air, earth, and water, and they are yellow bile, phlegm, blood, and black bile. So these are four, not fourfold; so it has the power of the natures and their equilibrium. And this noble letter appeared in His تعالى name al-Da'im especially and in His name al-Wadud. And nothing preceded in al-Da'im except dal, and for that reason the two noble, generous names were Ahmad and Muhammad. And it indicates that دوام is the last end of its beginning, so it is after dal for دوام. Rather, it was advanced in His name al-Da'im because He has الديمومة first and last, so He made His servants partners in the دوام of remaining after annihilation in the Hereafter. And this letter is for the Throne because the Throne does not change its existence, since it is the first world of inventions, and it is the first world, and to it are the ascents of the spirits, and in it are the ranks of the intellects, and in it are the lights of mercy. And some of those who know Allah تعالى have disclosed that upon the oath which was sworn for Harithah رضي الله تعالى عنه when the Messenger of Allah صلى الله عليه وسلم asked him and said: How have you أصبحْتَ, O Harithah?

So Harithah said: O Messenger of Allah, I have أصبحْتُ a true believer. So the Messenger of Allah صلى الله عليه وسلم said: What is the reality of your faith?

So Harithah said: I أصبحْتُ and I had presented myself to the world, and its stone and its gold, its living and its dead, its rich and its poor, were equal to me, and as though I were looking at the Throne of the Merciful exposed, while the people are being driven to the reckoning and to Paradise and to the Fire. So the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم said to him: You have known, so adhere. And the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم said concerning the spirits:

if they become manifest upon purity from ablution, then they remain prostrating beneath the Throne. (And the letter dal) has from the secrets permanence and remaining. (As for al-Wadud) it is a name from love, and love is shared, and it is the outward love, and love is the inward of love, and the first of love is affection. And love is divided into two parts, outward and inward; its outward is love and its inward is affection. So love, its dwelling is the heart, and it is the most revealing of the worlds of the heart, and al-shaqq is a subtlety between love and love; its dwelling is al-shaghaf, and the most beloved inward of passion and its dwelling is the fu'ād, because the heart has three cavities, one of them in its upper part, from what is thickened of it, and it is a shining light, and it is the place

[p. 13]

The place of Islam and the meanings of the letters, there is a problem there, and it is also the place of the speaking power in the human being, managing the meanings of the will emanating from the soul, and the second in the middle of the heart, and it is the place of reflection and remembrance, and it is a shining light, and it is the place of tranquility and the place of imagination in what the spirit casts, and the third in its end, and it is its most delicate and most subtle, and it is expressed by the fu'ād, and it is the place of faith and intellect and light and disposition and secrets and the balance of intellect and the subtleties of wisdom and the place of the life of nature from the subtle heat, and for this fu'ād there is a luminous eye by which it perceives the realities of the malakūtī things and the secrets of the higher things, the particulars and the universals and the balances of realities, and it is the place of the gifted lights and the secrets of the higher things, and that insight by which it looks, and it is that concerning which God, تعالى, said: ﴿ فَإِنَّهَا لَا تَعْمَى الْأَبْصَارُ وَلَكِن تَعْمَى الْقُلُوبُ الَّتِي فِي الصُّدُورِ﴾ and the middle cavity, which is the place of love, is a luminous eye by which the seeking is perceived, and from it earnestness in the seeking and longing for the sought thing is stirred, and it is quicker in attachment to persons because of its subtlety, and by it the world of mulk and what it contains of the making of God, تعالى, is uncovered, and by it approval occurs for the approved things, then for the first cavity there is a luminous eye by which one looks to the secrets of the sensible things and the states of the composites and the realities of the letters and their secrets and the عظیم what God, تعالى, deposited in them of the secrets of the names and the realities of their knowledges, and by it there was a gift to the servants of God, تعالى, and for magnifying God, تعالى, by knowing Him through it and by what God bestowed upon it of its uncovering the secrets of the sensible things, and those are the insights of all the hearts, except that they are differing in the اختلاف of matters. And there has preceded in the times of the insights and the subtleties of the inner secrets that the spirits of revelation in the Book of God are three: the Spirit of the Trustworthy One, and the Spirit of Holiness, and the Spirit of the Command. So the Spirit of the Trustworthy One يدل upon the first cavity because it is the isthmian one that is between speech and the tongue, so it is the first of the ranks of revelation in the sending down, each according to what God apportioned for him of inspiration and revelation upon the hearts. And after it is the Spirit of Holiness, and it is the lights of what comes down in the Preserved Tablet to the second rank of the heart, so faith and the intellectual insight are established, and the lights of wisdom and the lights of the resources and the faith subtleties appear. Then the second rank, and it is the place of the Most Holy, and it is also the place of hearing, and it is the place of intellect. He, تعالى, said to His Prophet, صلى الله عليه وسلم: ﴿ إِنَّكَ لَا تُسْمِعُ الْمَوْتَى وَلَا تُسْمِعُ الصُّمَّ الدُّعَاءَ﴾ and that was not intended by it to mean the death of the sense, but rather He intended by it the death of unbelief and disobedience, and He did not intend by deafness the ears, because the faculty of hearing is present, but rather He intended by this hearing which is in the fu'ād, and it is the place of intellect, and it is the place of the descent of the Spirit of the Command, which indicates enabling and the reality of union, and none was singled out by this descent except Muhammad, صلى الله عليه وسلم. And we have explained the medicines of the hearts and their treasuries and their lights and their insights in our book called Mawaqif al-Ghayat fi Asrar al-Riyadat, so review it; you will find it tested, if God, تعالى, wills. ﴿ He, تعالى, said﴾ ﴿ إِنَّ الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا وَعَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ سَيَجْعَلُ لَهُمُ الرَّحْمَنُ وُدًّا﴾ that is, there will be found in their hearts an affection taking hold of their totalities, so they love Him. And that is because they love their hearts with the kinds of remembrances and the states of the recitations, so they do not leave from the works of the heart that by which they are not supported, and they do not love their selves except by cutting off habits and familiar things until there is obtained for it affection from God, تعالى, then its speech turns into the speaking of wisdom and its movement into the ascent of degrees, and he loves his spirit with the realities of faith and the الدقيقة الإسلامية and the light of the الشرعية secrets and the religious lights until the آثار of affection appear upon the spirit, so it looks to the return, unveiling what God, تعالى, has prepared of kinds of bliss for His friends and punishment for His enemies, so its nature increases in seeking the return to God, تعالى, and longing for Him, and its intellect increases in reflection upon the made things in what God deposited of the secrets of His signs, and he loves it by leaving all

[p. 14]

The relations and the familiarities, and he loves him by complying with his command, and he is upon purity, and he commands only with the beneficial good. So when the heart turns toward love, it returns looking into the secrets of the wonders of the kingdom, then the states of the addresses, and the divine revelation, and the upper realities. And let us return to what we were engaged in, of the letter Dāl and its benefits: whoever writes the letter Dāl thirty-five times, and it is the number falling upon it, and writes with it the form of the square on a white silk cloth, and the moon in its house, محفوظاً from Jupiter, and around it the letter Dāl thirty-five times, put it in the interior of the ring at that time and wear it upon complete purity and fasting and inward clarity—may God تعالى perpetuate upon him provision and good. And whoever increases in mentioning his everlasting name, that is obtained for him. And we have mentioned a body of the properties of his everlasting name and the Dāl from praise in our book “The Science of Guidance and the Secrets of Being Guided,” so review it. (And whoever draws it) in this form and carries it with him attains all aims with the kings and the rulers, and whoever sees him loves him, and he attains the abundant fortune. And this is its writing on a yellow silk cloth, and the moon is in the house of Cancer or in the house of Jupiter, محفوظاً from it, and fumigate it with every thing of pleasant scent, and this is its description.

(And some of them mentioned) that whoever writes “Muhammad is the Messenger of God, صلى الله عليه وسلم, Ahmad is the Messenger of God, صلى الله عليه وسلم” thirty-five times on a card after the Friday prayer and carries it with him, God تعالى provides him with strength for obedience and assistance for blessing, and suffices him the stings of the devils. And if he continues looking at this card while he imagines the name of the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم Muhammad and his name Ahmad, and how the two noble, exalted, honored, venerated names were completed by this noble letter, which is the Dāl, and he continues looking at it at sunrise every day while he prays upon the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم, God makes easy for him the means of obedience and the greatest happiness, and that according to acceptance and truthfulness of intention and purity of inwardness, and it is a very subtle secret. (And whoever writes) its numerical form and carries it, God تعالى secures him from the harmful enemies, from whatever worlds they may be. (And whoever writes it) and erases it and gives it to drink to one who complains of an all-encompassing fever, that benefits him greatly. And since this square form is with the total of the four alifs, which are the secret of the intellect and the secret of the spirit and the secret of the soul and the secret of the heart, then the alif in the number is one, and if the four are multiplied by themselves they reach sixteen, and it is the end of the detailed number, because the Throne and the Footstool and the seven heavens and the seven earths, the total, [is] sixteen. And this number is the end of this square form, which is sixteen; the sixteen is composed of fourteen pairs, and they are the seven heavens and the seven earths, and in it is the pair of the signs, and they are twelve, and in it is the pair of the eighth, and they are the bearers of the Throne, and in it is the pair of six, and they are the eight limits, and they are above and below and behind and before and right and left, and in it is the pair of four, and they are the pair of the prophets and the truthful and the martyrs and the righteous, and in it is the pair of the two verses, and they are the testimony that there is no god except God and that Muhammad is the Messenger of God, peace be upon him. So these are seven pairs. And in it is from the oddness the odd of fifteen, and they are the Footstool and the heavens and the earths. And in it is the odd of thirteen, and they are the Pen and the Tablet and the Sur and the Holy Spirit and the Footstool and the Throne and the seven heavens. And in it is the odd of eleven, and they are what is in the world of man of the five senses, and they are hearing and sight and smell and taste and touch, and the six directions, and above and below and right and left and behind and before. And in it is the oddness of nine, and they are the entities of man and his eight natures, and they are heat and dryness and coldness and moisture. So the yellow bile is hot, dry, and the air is hot, moist, and it is the nature of blood, and phlegm, its nature is cold, moist, and black bile is cold, dry. So these are eight separate. And in it is the odd of seven, and it is the odd of the seven spheres: the sphere of Saturn, and Jupiter, and Mars, and the Sun, and Venus, and Mercury, and the Moon; and the odd of the seven days, and the seven heavens, and the seven earths, and every sevenfold. And in it is the odd of the pairs, and they are the five prayers. And in it is the odd of three, and they are the three abodes: the abode of the world, and the abode of the barzakh, and the abode of the Hereafter. And in it is the odd of one, and it is the odd of the intellect.

So gather in the sixteen eight pairs and eight odds, and every pair receives as an odd and as an odd, and every pair receives * Example of that: one, one, two, and three, and six, and thus to its end, and likewise the pairs. * And as for the numerical form with the natural pen, and it is the Indian, according to what the explanation of it will come, and this pen is more fitting for it and more deserving, and in letters there are wondrous secrets. And that is that you place a form and in the place of the numbers letters, and it is after a fast of two weeks, because every one of them is a night except the part alone, on the condition of discipline and the دوام of purity and the remembrance of God Most High. Then proceed to a square sheet of refined tin, engraved upon it this literal form, while you are facing the qiblah, after two rak‘ahs of prayer with al-Fatiha and Ayat al-Kursi once and al-Ikhlas one hundred times, in the hour of Jupiter at sunrise, and the moon is محفوظ from Jupiter and the sun and the rising sign of Gemini. And fumigate it with mastic and white sandalwood every Thursday. So the wearer of this ring, God makes easy for him the affairs of the world and the Hereafter and religion, and He grants him success for righteous deeds and acts of obedience, and the means of provision are made easy for him, and God Most High blesses what is between his hands. — Chapter Two —
Chapter Two — On Fraction and Expansion
الفصل الثاني في الكسر والبسط وترتيب الأعمال في الأوقات والساعات
On fraction and expansion, and the arrangement of works in the times and hours

[p. 16]

And the obtaining of blessing upon what comes after it, if God Most High wills. And (whoever writes it) on Thursday on gazelle parchment at sunrise and carries it in the sewn seam of his clothes, he is secured, by the help of God Most High, from thieves and all afflictions and everything that is feared and guarded against. And it is the first of the subjects of numbers, and I shall inform you of something of the secrets of numbers and what God has manifested in them, and the description of their benefits and harms and their transformations, and the secret of the dotted letters that are in the Book of God Most High, and they are the beginnings of the surahs, which are twenty-eight surahs, upon which none looks except the elect of His creation, and what is in the names of God, which are the treasuries of secrets and the courses of decrees, and the knowledge of the name of God, the greatest, the grandest, and what is in it of the divine secrets and the attributes of lordship, what you will not find in a book nor come upon in a register, from what I have gathered of the pearls of the sciences and the obscure secrets, so that he who reads it and understands its meaning may benefit by it, if God Most High wills. And this is its form and its description, as you see. And it has a great supplication composed from its form and its letters: ا ب ج د هـ و ز ح ط ي, the yā’ being added to it, and it is the tenth, and from them this noble invocation was arranged, and it is this:

O God, I ask You by all Your most beautiful names, what I know of them and what I do not know: O He, O One, O Single, O Guide, O Lord, O Creator, O All-Seeing, O Inventive, O Expander, O Remaining, O Majestic, O Everlasting, O Inheritor, O Loving, O Living, O Wise, O Forbearing, O Manifest, O One who makes manifest, answer my supplication and fulfill my need, O Lord of the worlds. (And since it has preceded) that the letters are twenty-eight according to the number of the twenty-eight mansions, the apparent of them above the earth is fourteen mansions and beneath the earth fourteen; so when a mansion has risen, its fifteenth counterpart has risen, and thus forever. Therefore the letters are fifteen dotted and fourteen without dots. And the dotted ones are thus: ب ث ج خ ذ ز ش ض ظ غ ف ق ن, and the undotted ones are thus: ا ح د ر س ص ط ع ك ل م هـ و لا. (And know) may God grant us and you success, that the undotted letters are the mansions of the fortunes and the dotted letters are the mansions of the misfortunes, and what has one dot is closer to fortune, and what has two dots is intermediate in the misfortunes, and what has three is a greater misfortune, like the shīn and the tā’, so reflect on that. (And know) that the mansions have different forms of placement in the divine circle, not resembling one another, and the moon God created round, and likewise the sun, for a hidden secret that cannot be explained, because divulging the secret of lordship is unbelief. So when the moon descends in the mansion of al-naṭḥ, it has indications that point to it, and a long discourse, some of which will come, but walls have ears, and the best thing in this is concealment, so understand what I have indicated and reflect on it, you will be guided.

Chapter Two on breaking and expansion and the arrangement of works in the times and hours

( Know ) may God grant me and you success in obedience to Him and understanding the secrets of His names, that the sun and the moon God Most High mentioned in His Mighty Book by His saying, Most High: each in an orbit are swimming. That is because the moon, when it is in the mansion of al-naṭḥ, has the letter alif, and it is with the secret of alif. So when it descends the mansion, the spirituality of alif becomes manifest from that mansion, and anger appears in the parts of the world, and most of it is in the nobles of the people of the world, and greatest of it, so every one of creation finds coercion and anger in his inward according to the kind in which you inspect that, you find it. So it is fitting for a person to be still in that hour and occupy his limbs in the worship of God Most High and much supplication and remembrance and adherence to purity during that period.

And say:

| ا | يه | يد | د | |---|---|---|---| | يب | و | ز | ط | | يج | ج | ب | يو |

[p. 17]

And before the period, because in it there occurs some embittering of souls, so that the human being does not know what is the cause of his constriction, and he becomes wondering within himself. And that is because the alif is the first rank of the ones in numbers and letters, so there is no doubt in it for him. And therefore disturbance occurred in the lower world, so understand that. And in it is embittering of whomever you want to embitter and constrict, from the people of the world, from the people of domineering and arrogance, for it suits his embittering and constriction, because in the letter alif there is heat and dryness, and it is the face of the red one, and the red one is hot, dry; his nature is fire, burning, imprisoning. So if you call in it with hot, dry names from its nature, when the moon is in al-Natḥ, rising on the eastern horizon, and the moon is in it, and what was mentioned is correct ﵆. And whoever writes the letter alif one hundred times and eleven times in red copper or iron or a potsherd of pottery, cast it upon the name of whomever you want to embitter, and bury it in his house after fumigating it with something of its kind, and there should be in it heat like the letter, from what corresponds to it, and call with the names one hundred and eleven times, and they are the numbers falling upon it. That is, you take the letters of the name of whomever you want to embitter, and spread them out, and look at what predominates over him in his name from the four natures, which are heat and dryness, coldness and moisture. Then you take those hot and dry letters from his name and place them before you in a tablet, and add to them the letters of Mars and the Moon, and make from them a name from the names of God تعالى, and call by it the number mentioned, and gather your resolve in his mouth, and his subjugation will be that ﵆. Its example: Zayd and ʿAmr, so place the letters thus: m r w m d y kh n ṭ ḥ q m r, separated, spread out. So these are fourteen letters, among them fiery, airy, earthy, and watery, and they are w y n. And among the moist letters there is one letter, and it is the letter q. So the repeated hot letters were four: m m ṭ. And the dry letters are letters, namely ḥ ع d d ḥ. And the hot are three letters, and the moist one.

Thus the fourteen letters were confined here, so the predominant over these letters was heat and the heat of dryness. So there emerged for us from the names of God تعالى this ʿazīmah: you say, O Samsāʾīl, by Him who created you, then fashioned you, and made you a light in His sphere, except that you were my provision, for I have empowered you over 652 and a helper to me in what I desire of vengeance against so-and-so and so-and-so, and the loss of his senses, and you mix with the heat of Mars in the heat of his nature, and you stir in him the heat of fire by subduing his joints and you constrict with it upon his belly, and you wrap with it his intellect, and you leave upon him the torment and the fire of Mars, and you move the fires and the headache and the rest of the pains, by the right of Mars and what is in it of ill-omen and fire, and by the right of your lofty, high-valued station, dry, hot, avenging from the الظلمة, the tyrannical and the transgressors. And send to him the Ruhāniyyah of this chosen one, the tyrannical, the arrogant, the transgressor, and settle in his body from the torment of ailments, and empower over his inward part subjugation, wrath, and vengeance, for I have sworn upon you by the powerful, the encompassing, the manifest, the living, the self-subsisting, the light, the faithful, the advancing, the delaying, the effuser of lights, and the giver of secrets, and by the right of fire and sparks and the red star, and by the right of God, the One, the Subduer: respond, obedient, hastening, to the names of the Lord of the worlds. (The first descent) al-Sharṭayn, and it has the letter alif, and it has a compliant wafq, and its planet is Mars, and its servant is the red one, and it is a letter of strong efficacy; if you strike it in its like, it manifests obedience, and it is the end of the ones. (And know) that this noble letter has power in the disposition of the rest of the letters, because it is like the alif, so understand. (And among its properties) for love:

write kābāynāh in a fortunate hour, and if you mix the name of the person for whom you want to work with the letters of the wafq, it is more beautiful and stronger in the acts. And this ʿazīmah says: I have sworn upon you, O Samsāʾīl, and your servants and your helpers

[p. 18]

From the upper and the lower, and the servants of the letter Alif all together, except what you have heard and obeyed and stirred up, so-and-so and so-and-so, and by the right of what I have sworn to you by, and by the right of the letter Alif and what God has sent down in it of the secrets which no one becomes aware of except those who know God, تعالى, and by the right of Abjad and what is in it of the properties, except what you answered with obedience as I called you to it, and by what I swore to you by, and this is its form as you see, so understand, you will be guided.

And measure on this what is suitable to it of the works and the acts; your work will succeed, and God is the one who grants success to what is right.

And if you want for works of corruption, then do as we mentioned first: of hatred, destruction, ruin, and what resembles that, and measure upon it and do [it], you will attain the intended. And God says the truth, and He guides the way.

And this is the invocation of the letter Alif, with which you invoke the preceding number: Glory be to You, there is no god except You, Lord of everything and its inheritor, and the earth.

The second station) the station of al-Batīn, and it is for the letter Bā’; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it, by the command of God تعالى, a spiritual force suitable for anger, and what was mentioned before in it: the medicine is drunk, and in it the great ones, and the sons of the world, and the kings of the earth move, because it is the second face of Aries, and it is the face of the sun, and in it is its nobility in nineteen degrees of it on the fourth day of April, and the sun is fortunate, except that it is hot, dry; so its fortune and its nobility in this face: in it you work for acceptance and entry upon kings and what is intended from it, for needs are fulfilled. And work for affections and acceptance and bringing of hearts; be found by it. And it is suitable for the work of الحكمي الصناعات and the golden elixirs and their management. (The third station) the station of الثريا, and it has the letter Jīm; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a spiritual force mixed with heat and moisture and coldness, and a medium fortune, good for travel, and mixed with the nobles and entry upon the great ones and the lords of the world and the people of the pen, because الثريا is gathered together in abundance from the stars.

And therefore the conjunction, with what we mentioned, was good, and for it a great Wafq, worked in its nobility and carried, and by it Abu Ja‘far al-Barmaki was acceptable with al-Rashid and obtained from him what he desired; for its bearer, if he enters with it upon kings and the great ones, obtains his desire from them, and no one مخالفة him in what he desires. And this is its form, so understand, you will be guided, and God knows best.

The fourth mansion) the mansion of al-Dabaran, and for it

the letter dal; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it an evil Ruhaniyyah, working in it what is fitting for it of harmful works and فساد, and what is fitting for that.

ha; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah mixed with the moderate hot, working in it works of good and some of them of its opposite.

The sixth mansion) the mansion of al-Han‘ah, and for it the letter waw, and it is a fortunate mansion, suitable for affection and conjunction between those far apart, because

there descends with it a good Ruhaniyyah, assisting illnesses upon treatments, assisting upon works of piety, righteousness, and success. (The mansion seventh) the mansion of al-Dhira‘, and for it the letter zay; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a good Ruhaniyyah, assisting illnesses in treatments, and perhaps whoever persists in its remembrance, there is opened for him something of the malakut. And it is good for retreats and seeking the truth, and it is suitable for all works. (The eighth mansion) the mansion of al-Nathrah, and for it the letter ha; if the moon descends in it, there is manifested from it a Ruhaniyyah not assisting toward good, working in it matters of فساد. (The ninth mansion) the mansion of al-Tarfah, and for it the letter ta; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah like the preceding. (The tenth mansion) the mansion of al-Jabhah, and for it the letter ya; if the moon descends, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah mixed between good and evil; do in it what is fitting for it. (The eleventh mansion) the mansion of al-Zubrah, and for it the letter kaf; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah suitable for the growth of provisions and the seeking of needs; do in it what is fitting for it. (The twelfth mansion) al-Sarfah, and for it the letter lam; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah mixed for good and evil; do in it what is fitting for it. (The thirteenth) the mansion of al-‘Awa’ and for it the letter mim; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah in which nothing moves except for riding the sea, no other than that.

The fourteenth mansion) the mansion of al-Samak, and for it the letter nun; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah that does not assist toward

good, so do not do in it anything at all. (The fifteenth mansion) the mansion of al-Ghafr, and for it the letter sin; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a good Ruhaniyyah assisting upon all worldly and otherworldly movements; so do in it whatever you wish, your work will succeed. (The sixteenth mansion) the mansion of al-Zubana, and for it the letter ‘ayn; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a mixed Ruhaniyyah; nothing moves in it except good. (The seventeenth mansion) the mansion of al-Iklil, and for it the letter fa; if the moon descends in it, there descends

From it is a Ruhaniyyah not specified for the doing of good; so act in it with what is suitable from the affairs of the world, the righteous, you will prosper. (The eighteenth mansion) Mansion of al-Qalb, and it has the letter ṣād. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a Ruhaniyyah that aids in the acts of good; so act in it with what is suitable from the righteous deeds. (The nineteenth mansion) Mansion of al-Shawlah, and it has the letter qāf. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a mixed Ruhaniyyah; so let nothing of the affairs of the world move in it. (The twentieth mansion) Mansion of al-Naʿāʾim, and it has the letter rāʾ. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a mixed, pure Ruhaniyyah that purifies the hearts and makes the النفس rejoice, good for everything that is attempted in it from the affairs of the world and the Hereafter. (The twenty-first mansion) Mansion of al-Baldah, and it has the letter shīn. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a mixed Ruhaniyyah; it is not suitable for anything of the affairs of the world, nor benefit for movement in it, nor harm. (The twenty-second mansion) Mansion of Saʿd al-Dhābiḥ, and it has the letter tāʾ. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a mixed Ruhaniyyah; it is not suitable for anything of the affairs of the world, nor benefit for movement in it, nor harm. (The twenty-third mansion) Mansion of Saʿd Bulaʿ, and it has the letter thāʾ. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a Ruhaniyyah of moderate nature; perform in it all works of good deeds. (The twenty-fourth mansion) Mansion of Saʿd al-Suʿūd, and it has the letter ḥāʾ. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a happy Ruhaniyyah for movement, of moderate nature, aiding all the acts of good; so do in it whatever you want from works of good. (The twenty-fifth mansion) Mansion of Saʿd al-Akhbiyah, and it has the letter dhāl. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a happy Ruhaniyyah aiding all the acts of good; so do by it whatever you desired from works of good. (The twenty-sixth mansion) Mansion of al-Farʿ al-Muqaddam, and it has the letter ḍād. If the moon settles in it, from it descends a happy Ruhaniyyah aiding all the acts of good; so do in it whatever you want. (The twenty-seventh mansion) Mansion of al-Farʿ al-Muʾakhkhar, and it has the letter ẓāʾ. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a mixed Ruhaniyyah; in it is followed trial and causes. (The twenty-eighth mansion) Mansion of al-Rashā, and it has the letter ghayn. If the moon descends in it, from it descends a good, praiseworthy, pleasant Ruhaniyyah aiding in the seeking of the sciences, and supplication in it is answered to response, and the righteous deeds in it are complete. So look, my brother, at what God تعالى established by the letters of benefits. And since from them the speech of God تعالى is composed, and by them the names of God تعالى are disposed, and by them one understands His address, the meaning that is in their inward is the Ruhaniyyah descending from the mansions. And just as the عظیم Qurʾān فيه verses of mercy and verses of punishment, the verses of mercy were the angels of good fortune in respect of the one shown mercy by them, and the verses of punishment the angels of punishment are felt by the one punished by them, and verses necessitating promise and threat; those are what are expressed by the mixed Ruhaniyyah. And that is only in respect of the human being, and there is no deficiency in respect of the angels, and they are pure good. And it does not negate that the human being is pure good: he is the name قائم by him, and pure evil, and he is the disbeliever, and mixed good, and he is the sinful believer, those concerning whom God تعالى said: “And others who acknowledged their sins, they mixed a righteous deed and another evil one; perhaps God will turn to them.” So this is the mainstay of the secrets in the letters: the cycles turned around the point upon the manifestation of composition, to the cycles of the zodiac signs, to the world, every mansion and every Ruhaniyyah and every letter gathered in the point in forty days, then likewise to the end of the mansions. So the last of the letters are letters of Ruhaniyyah that gather the fortunes and the misfortunes. Were it not for this lettered differentiation and the celestial cycle, man would not have known the causes of happiness from wretchedness, from the misfortunes, and the causes of mixture from lagging behind; and all that is branched from the children of Adam.

Section

[p. 21]

Chapter) And when these mansions were in need of the twelve signs of the zodiac so that His wisdom might appear in them, the twelve letters were in six divisions, and they are the letters of “There is no god but Allah,” thus: ل ا ل ا ه ا ل ا ل ا ل اه, and they are twelve letters according to the number of the twelve signs of the zodiac; in them every sign establishes. And when the signs are among them fixed and among them mutable, likewise these letters are among them fixed and among them mutable; so affirmation and negation are mutable from existence to nonexistence, which is from it. And these circular letters are not among them the sphere of the moon, because the moon is nearer to the earth than other than it, and the letters are nearer to us than the moon because they are implanted in the nature of every human being. And the letters have been mentioned before the mansions, so it has sufficed from repeating them. And everything increases with the increase of the moon and decreases with its decrease, wisdom and knowledge of its rank. Do you not see how darkness increases and other than it? ۝ And when the seven planets were, Allah made in them the secret of guidance, according to His saying تعالى: making in the earth a vicegerent, and His saying تعالى: making the angels messengers. And the powers of these seven planets are taken from the powers of the inward divisions, and they are “There is no god but Allah”; so these are derived from these exalted sanctities. And these letters, the hot and the moist and the cold and the dry: the hot are seven letters, and they are ا هـ ط م ف ش ذ; and the moist are seven letters ب و ي ن س ت ض; and the cold are seven likewise ج ز ث ص ق ث ظ; and the dry likewise د ح ل ر خ غ. And the explanation of that is that fire is a gatherer of heat and dryness, and air is a gatherer of moisture and heat, and water is a gatherer of moisture and cold, and earth is a gatherer of dryness and cold. And the four natures mentioned have become, namely yellow bile and blood and phlegm and black bile. So yellow bile is the nature of fire, dry; and blood is the nature of air, hot, moist; and black bile is the nature of earth, cold, dry; and phlegm is the nature of water, cold, moist. And the effect of that has appeared by sight, and that is that some names repel fever by writing, and they are the cold and dry names, such as al-‘adl and al-shadid; one enters them in a Mabsu‘. And some names repel the cold of death, and they are the burning yellow bile. And this is the form of its square as you see; so understand, you will be guided.

and their hours, and what of them accords with good and evil) (On Sunday). The first hour is for the sun; work in it for love and acceptance and entering upon kings and rulers, and it is suitable in it for wearing new clothes. The second hour is for Venus, and it is an blameworthy hour; do not do in it anything at all of all things. The hour third is for Mercury; travel in it and write in it for affection and love and speech and what resembles them. The fourth hour is for the moon; there is no benefit in it in anything, and do not buy, and it is not suitable for anything. The fifth hour is for Saturn; work in it for separation and hatred and enmity and the like . The sixth hour is for Jupiter; seek in it needs from kings. The seventh hour is for Mars; do not work in it anything . The eighth hour is for the sun; work in it all needs, for it is suitable for all matters and it is very fortunate. The hour ninth is for Venus; write in it for bringing people and inclining hearts and what resembles that. The tenth hour is for Mercury

And I have set down for you a table from which you know the fiery, earthy, airy, and watery signs. So if the moon is in the fiery signs, then do for it what accords with it from the works of fire, and likewise the rest of the signs. So know what has become to you, and this is its description: if a seeker of a need comes to you on any day whatsoever, then write his name and his mother’s name and the name of his sought thing, separated letters, and arrange the third upon its element. If it is in a fiery, earthy, airy, or watery sign, then do for him a work that accords with it; otherwise delay the work until the moon enters the sign that is sought. If that time agrees, then its owner is fortunate. So know that, and God knows best. (And this is a great rule in knowing the sign of the moon), which is that you add what has passed of the Arabic month and add five to it, then give to each sign هـ هـ, beginning from the sign of the sun; so wherever the number is exhausted, that is the sign of the moon, which it is from. And God knows best. (2 - Shams al-Ma'arif - First)

— Chapter Three —
Chapter Three — On the Twenty-Eight Lunar Mansions
الفصل الثالث في أحكام منازل القمر الثمانية والعشرين الفلكيات
On the rulings of the twenty-eight lunar mansions

[p. 24]

-- 18 -- ﴿ Chapter on the concealment of the angels of the letters, without which the work is not completed ﴾ And it is: if you desire a work, then look into the letters of the name of the seeker and the sought, and the name of that day, and subtract them 33, and if there remains without them then the last of the letters, the concealment will be for that letter itself, and it is not possible for the owners of the names to be absent from that, the twinkling of an eye. This is from the most of works, and this is the description of the concealment of the angels: the angel of alif, Talhatayā’īl, and its concealment is these letters: Hadhayūb Samtā Yāsamkhalaq; the angel of bā’; its concealment: Tasīkh Halīīj; Mars; the angel of jīm; its concealment: Mahlīīj Salāk Bihalūh; the angel of dāl; its concealment: Muḥṭamatka; the angel of hā’; its concealment: Maḥṭamalak; the angel of rā’; its concealment: Mahlūh; Salīmūḥ Bārākh; the angel of zāy; its concealment: Saʿdabūāh; Ṭalṭam; the angel of ḥā’; its concealment: Laylā Ṭalḥ; the angel of ṭā’; its concealment: Shamhaṭ Salīsāḥ Ṭamh; the angel of yā’; its concealment: Maqh; Hakahf; Suwaydaḥ; the angel of kāf; its concealment: Sabʿūdah; Naftā; Madīḥ; the angel of lām its concealment: ʿAṭīṭ; Ṭamsh; the angel of nūn; its concealment: Madīḥ; Kalīl; the angel of sīn; its concealment: Ḥamṭ; Maṭlaʿ; Mamlaṭ; Jism the angel of ʿayn; its concealment: Lajlīm; ʿan Fawādir; the angel of fā’; its concealment: Kayṭam; Warṭsh; Hafīẓ; the angel of ṣād; its concealment: Masʿūd; Hamīsh; the angel of qāf; its concealment: ʿAdʿaqīr; al-Ṭalḥayāsh; the angel of zāy; its concealment: Saṭīt; Halīl; Dahyūm; the angel of shīn; its concealment: ʿAslasīn; Hahfaʿal; Maḥṭ; the angel of tā’; its concealment: Yamrīlūh; Hafīṭ; the angel of thā’; its concealment: Maḥfaṭ; the angel of ḥā’ its concealment: Hajīḥ; Hahyahal; the angel of dhāl; its concealment: ʿĀmiṣ; Muḥad; Salīṭ; the angel of ḍād; its concealment: ʿĀlamiṣ; Ṣahdaʿ; Shabāṭ the angel of ẓā’; its concealment: Nūʿ; Radgh; Ahmūsh; Ahmūsh. ﴿ And know ﴾ that the angel of ḍād and the angel of ṭā’ have for them a single one, its concealment and take the angel of ghayn; its concealment: Saʿaltu Kalakat Ahyūdh; and Niʿmat; and the total of the concealments is these. And Allah, Glorified and Exalted is He, knows best what is right. And may Allah bless our master Muḥammad, and upon his family and his companions, and grant peace.

﴿ The third chapter concerning the rulings of the twenty-eight lunar mansions ﴾ Know — may Allah grant me and you success in obedience to Him — that after that there are matters from which is known the crescent of every month to every mansion, and it is that you look at the last day of the Arabic month, for the sun and the moon will be in one mansion, then at the end of that day, whether it is in the first third of the Roman month or the second or the third or the first half or the second, and enter with the elapsed of the Arabic month under that third or half; you will find the mansion in which the moon is, so know it. Then enter with the elapsed of the Coptic upon the mansions, and you will know the mansion in which it is. Its example: is the crescent the first night with al-sharṭayn, and seven days have passed from the Arabic so we wanted to know the mansion in which the moon is on that day, so we counted from al-sharṭayn seven mansions, and we ended at al-dhirāʿ, so we knew that the moon is at al-dhirāʿ; and measure on that. And this is the description of the circle, it is seen, so understand, you will be guided.

(القول

[p. 25]

The discourse on the mansions and their forms and what is connected with them of rulings ) The discourse on the mansion of al-Sharṭayn, and this

is its description: هـ هـ and it has the letter alif. If the moon descends in al-Sharṭayn, and it is fiery, inauspicious, there are done in it of the works what was specific to the affairs of the world and corruption and shedding of blood. And the wise men in this time used to incline to sleep and lack of movement.

And some of them mentioned that he sees in his sleep what frightens him and troubles his character, and that lack of sleep in this time is a benefit. And if you desire a work, then do in it the works of evil for its deserving one. And whoever is born in this mansion will be much corrupt, and its incense pepper and black seed, and God knows best. ( The discourse on the mansion of al-Buṭayn ) And this is هـ هـ and it has the letter bā’. If the moon descends in al-Buṭayn, and it is hot, moist, there descends in it to the world, by permission of God تعالى, a righteous Ruhaniyyah. It is suitable for what was specific to the affairs of men دون النساء, and do in it the talismans, and in it the alchemy is sound, and every craft of great worth. And it is suitable in it for the beginning of the sciences, and the craft of rings, and engraving, and numerals, and incantations for illnesses and treatment. And whoever is born in it lived happy, rightly guided, successful, beloved by creation, and its incense is oud and saffron and mastic, and God knows best. ( The discourse on the mansion of al-Thurayyā and this is its description ) هـ هـ هـ هـ and it has the letter jīm. If the moon descends in al-Thurayyā, there is generated from it, by permission of God تعالى, to the world a Ruhaniyyah mixed of heat and cold. Talismans are done in it, and what is suitable for women, and the management of the suitable medicines and coldness, and it is suitable for travelers, and they hope for increased profit, and it is suitable for kings and marriage and buying slave girls and slaves. And whatever is managed in it was good because it is the moon’s adjustment without the sun, and whatever is made in it was praiseworthy.

[p. 26]

The outcome: whoever is born in it lived happy and hated evil, and was praiseworthy in outcome; and whoever is born in it lived happy and hated debauchery and loves the righteous, and its incense is flax seed and black seed, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the Manzilah of al-Dabaran, and this is its description) ... And it has the letter dal. If the moon descends in al-Dabaran, and it is earthy, there descends in it to the world, by the permission of God Most High, a Ruhaniyyah that separates enmity and hatred and فساد in the earth. So beware in it of striving in seeking needs and beginning with الأعمال, and do not work in it a tilsam, and do not devise in it a craft, for all الأعمال in it are bad and do not suit except for burying the dead and burying money and concealing secrets and digging wells and cutting rivers, and they do not suit what is other than that. And whoever is born in it is blameworthy, forsaken, and its incense is sweet pomegranate peel and frankincense. (The saying concerning the Manzilah of al-Haqa‘ah) And this is its description ... And it has the letter ha. If the moon descends in it, and it is a mixture of fortunate and unlucky in it, then work in it on antidotes to poisons and their mixing especially, and do not devise in it a craft of the sun nor the moon, and do not plant in it a planting, and do not wear new [clothes], and do not marry in it, for its outcome is not praiseworthy. Its incense is agarwood and frankincense and Java and mastic, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the Manzilah of al-Haqa‘ah ... and this is its description) ... And it has the letter waw. If the moon descends in al-Haqa‘ah, and it is a fortunate star, then work in it for affection and love and amity, and go out in it with the fragrant scent, and enter in it upon the kings and the great ones, and strive in their needs and what you want, and معاشرة of the brothers, and begin in it with the الأعمال that you want, and marry in it, and drink the medicine in it, and buy in it the slave girls and the horses, and plant the trees, and build in it the building, measure, weigh, and travel in it, and sell and buy, for all that is very praiseworthy. And whoever is born in it lived happy and died a martyr. Its incense is qutrub and seed of sheeh, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the Manzilah of al-Dhirā‘, and this is its description) ... And it has the letter zay. If the moon descends in al-Dhirā‘, and it is a breezy fortunate, soft [Manzilah], there descends in it to the world, by the permission of God Most High, a good Ruhaniyyah. It is suitable in it to begin with the sciences and the righteous الأعمال and meeting with the scholars and the worshippers and the making of talismans and the doors of the orange-thingies and entering upon the kings and connecting with the nobles of the people and the brothers. And whoever is born in it was happy, rightly guided, successful. Its incense is seed of celery and flax seed. (The saying concerning the Manzilah of al-Nathrah, and this is its description) ... : And it has the letter ha. If the moon descends in it, and it is cold, mixed, fortunate, there descends in it to the world, by the permission of God Most High, a Ruhaniyyah that does in it enmity and hatred and severance and the like of that, and it is suitable for the making of talismans and supplication against enemies and tyrants and rebels and rancor, and a Ruhaniyyah moves in it, swifter in it, with the works of the instruments of war and the indication in it, because they are bad and suitable for the works of evil as we mentioned. And whoever is born in it was unlucky. Its incense is costus and pomegranate peel, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the Manzilah of al-Tarfah and this is its description) ... And it has the letter zha. If the moon descends in it, and it is a continuously unlucky star, there descends in it to the world a Ruhaniyyah that indicates the like of what has preceded. So do not work in it a tilsam, and do not devise in it a craft, and do not enter upon the kings, and do not begin in it with affection, and do not work in it wisdom, and do not fill an oath, and seclusion is better than mixing, and it is a rejection for all the الأعمال. And whoever is born in it will be unlucky. Its incense is frankincense and saffron, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the Manzilah of al-Jabhah and this is its description) ... And it has the letter ba. If the moon descends in it, and it is cold, unlucky, and it is closer to righteousness, one begins in it with the الأعمال of affection and the near الأعمال and satisfaction, and the transfer from place to place is suitable in it, and it is disliked in it the tailoring of iron and wearing it. And whoever is born in it will be skillful, happy, successful, but in it is some cunning and deceit. Its incense is seed

-- 21 -- Myrtle love and saffron, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the mansion of al-Ḥarthān, and this is its description): .. And it has the letter kāf. If the moon descends in it, and it is hot, dry, it is suitable for the treatments of the Ruhaniyyāt, and the making of talismans, and the treatment of the sick, and the healing of the disabled, and buying and selling, and entering upon kings and chiefs, and travel and residence are suitable in it, and the great works are suitable in it, and wearing new [clothes]. And whoever is born in it will be beloved among people, except that in it is some cunning and shrewdness, and its incense is pomegranate peel, ḥulūlan, and other than that, and God knows best.
