If the moon comes to it, and it is an earthy, dry planet, there descends in it to the world, by permission of God تعالى, a Ruhaniyyah that bequeaths enmity and corruption, and it is suitable for the works of deadly poisons, and everything that bequeaths corruption. Beginning good works is disliked in it, and buying and selling is disliked in it. And whoever is born in it was a liar, a tale-bearer, not praiseworthy in outcome. Its incense is male frankincense and ḥabb harmal, and God knows best.

The saying concerning the mansion of al-Ghafr, and this is its description). And it has the letter ʿayn. If the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah

that bequeaths love, affection, rest, and benefit from kings, and medicines are suitable in it, and that by which deadly poisons are dissolved and their harm is repelled in it, and it is suitable for the management of the honored stone, and Ruhaniyyāt are treated, and talismans are made in it. And whoever is born in it will be unlucky, one of cunning and deceit. Its incense is male frankincense only. (The saying concerning the mansion of al-Zubānā, and this is its description) h h And it has the letter ʿayn. If the moon descends in it, and it is a fortunate planet, there mixes in it for a true iron blow and for a dog bite, and for one concerning whom the enemies have spoken with evil speech, and an ailment will befall in his body from what pains him and tires him in its healing. And whoever is born in it will be fortunate in all his movements. Its incense is wormwood only, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the mansion of al-Iklīl, and this is its description) h h . And it has the letter fā’. If the moon descends in it, and it is a planet mixed of fortune and misfortune, there descends in it a Ruhaniyyah that draws tribulations and hatred and does evil in it and its opposite, so do not travel in it, and do not marry, and do not buy slaves, and do not plant trees, for its outcome is not praiseworthy, and do not tailor clothes in it, and do not dispute in it, and do not settle in it needs. And whoever is born in it will be bad, ominous. Its incense is pepper and saffron and oud, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the mansion of the heart and this is its description) h And it has the letter ṣād. If the moon descends in it, and it is a fortunate watery planet, a Ruhaniyyah descends from it that makes suitable what the preceding [mansion] has corrupted, and it is suitable for buying weapons and instruments of war, and buying beasts of burden and veterinary treatment, and cutting trees and crops and plowing, and bringing out buried treasure, and treating beasts, and drinking easy medicines, and phlebotomy and cupping. And whoever is born in it will be unlucky, male or female, but in it is some cunning. And its incense is the leaves of al-ihlīlaj, and God knows best.

The saying concerning the mansion of al-Shawlah, and this is its description) h h h And it has the letter qāf. If the moon descends in it, and it is a planet

[p. 28]

A happy one, in which there descends into the world, by the permission of God Most High, a mixed Ruhaniyyah that does evil in it and its opposite, and it is suitable for the place in it and the knots, and what was intermediate among the works. And the making of the new is disliked in it, and no talisman is worked in it, and the Ruhaniyyah are not treated in it. And solitude in it is praiseworthy. And whoever is born in it was base, ill-omened, a liar, a tale-bearer, a libertine. Its incense: pomegranate peel and mastic. (The saying concerning the station of al-Nu‘aim, and this is its description) . . . . . . And it has the letter ra’ if the moon descends in it, and it is a fiery, happy planet, unmixed, in which there descends into the world, blessed, happy, and it calls to affection and fortune and happiness, and it is praiseworthy in outcome in all conditions, and the management of honored industries is suitable in it, and judgment and admonitions and jurisprudential sciences and the making of talismans begin in it, and build a building, and plant the trees, and wear the new, for whoever wears it will remain in joy and gladness until it wears out. And whoever is born in it was blessed, happy, successful in all his movements. Its incense: frankincense. And God knows best. (The saying concerning the station of al-Balda, and this is its description) . . . . . . And it has the letter shin if the moon descends in it, and it is an inauspicious fiery planet; a Ruhaniyyah descends in it that works in it for enmity, hatred, severance, the honored stone, and the exalted jewel. And the Ruhaniyyah are not treated in it, and no planting is planted in it, and no travel is undertaken in it, and no mixing with kings, and no marriage, and do not buy slaves, and do not wear the new, and do not do any work from the works. And whoever is born in it will be unfortunate, a trickster. Its incense: spikenard and oud. And God knows best. (The saying concerning the station of Sa‘d al-Dhabih, and this is its description) . . . . . . And it has the letter tha’ if the moon descends in it, and it is an earthy, inauspicious, mixed planet; a Ruhaniyyah descends in it that works in it for hatred, enmity, and severance, and the outcomes of matters and works are not praised in it, and the kings are moved in it with anger and wrath, and buying and selling are completed in it, and digging, excavation, and agriculture are suitable in it, and hidden things and treasures come out in it, and secrets are concealed. And whoever is born in it was handsome, blessed, eager for the world, a trickster. Its incense: safflower. And God knows best. (The saying concerning the station of Sa‘d Bula‘, and this is its description) . . . . . . And it has the letter ta’ if the moon descends in it, and it is a mixed planet; there descends into the world in it, by the permission of God Most High, a Ruhaniyyah that does evil and its opposite, and it is the good and the bad. And it is suitable in it to buy slaves and mamluks, and it is suitable for buying beasts of burden and speaking with the shaykhs and the important matters of agriculture, and splitting rivers and digging wells and what is like that of hard works and fires and making foods. And whoever is born in it will be blessed, righteous. Its incense: chamomile. And God knows best. (The saying concerning the station of Sa‘d al-Su‘ud, and this is its description) . . . . . . And it has the letter ha’ if the moon descends in it, and it is a mixed planet from earth and air; a Ruhaniyyah descends in it that erases the last of what was before it, and all works are suitable in it. So begin in it with the making of love and affection and what is like that of mending hearts, and treat the Ruhaniyyah in it, and meet kings and chiefs and holders of offices and others, and do in it whatever you wish of acts of affection; your work will succeed. And whoever is born in it loves the righteous. Its incense: oud and mastic. And God knows best. (The saying concerning the station of Sa‘d al-Akhbiyah, and this is its description) . . . . . . And it has the letter dhal if the moon descends in it, and it is a windy planet; a Ruhaniyyah descends into the world in it that works in it for severance, seditions, hatred, division, and wars, and the works are not completed in it, and if they are completed they are not praiseworthy, and the sick are not treated in it, nor the Ruhaniyyah, and no talisman is worked in it, and no description of al-kimya and al-alsimya is managed in it. And whoever is born in it will be wicked, unbelieving. Its incense: frankincense, anzarut, and pepper. And God knows best. (The saying concerning the station of al-Fargh al-Muqaddam, and this is its description) . . . . . . And it has the letter dad.

[p. 29]

The Ḍād acts in it for love, and it stirs desire, and souls expand with affection, and it is suitable for the management of every quality and the treatment of Ruhaniyyah, and the setting up of talismans, and in it the deficient medicines are gathered, and enter upon kings and chiefs, and whoever is born in it, his end was praiseworthy; its incense is frankincense, black seed, and saffron, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the station of al-Fargh al-Mu’akhkhar, and this is its description) 0 0 And it has the letter ẓā’ if the moon descends in it, and it is a fortunate watery planet; in it succeeds, by permission of God تعالى, a Ruhaniyyah that manages in it the unpraised actions, such as advancing in the unlucky stations, and avoid in it war and meeting enemies and adversaries and shedding blood, and in it phlebotomy and cupping and the making of bleeding and the tying off from intercourse are suitable, and it is suitable for entering the bathhouse and taking the hair and the nail and drinking the beneficial medicines, and whoever is born in it was a wicked deceiver; its incense is pepper and Chinese cardamom, and God knows best. (The saying concerning the station of al-Rashā, and this is its description) 0 0 0 0: And for the letter ghayn if the moon descends in it, and it is a watery planet, in it descends to the world, by permission of God تعالى, a Ruhaniyyah praiseworthy in outcome; so work in it the talismans and the good works, and in it is managed the craft of the honored stone and the dark jewel, and treat in it the spiritual beings and all works in it are praiseworthy, and in it travel and marriage and wearing new clothes and moving from one place to another place and mixing with the wise and the chiefs are suitable, and whoever is born in it was blessed; its incense is black seed, and God knows best.

Chapter on the division of the stations upon the signs and the owner of the sign of the stations

Chapter on knowing the roots of the stations

This is a face other than the preceding, so you say its beginning is al-Sharṭayn, and it is two separated stars, one of them in the direction of the south and the other in the direction of the north, and they are the two horns of Aries, and the brighter of them is called al-Nāṭiḥ, and in the eye’s view, if the sky is midway, there is between them the amount of ten cubits, and near the two sky-poles is a small star that sometimes precedes them, and this is its form 0 0 0 And as for al-Buṭayn, they are three small stars, dim, strong in turning, and it is the belly of Aries; and it was only made small because the stars are many stars in the form of Aries, and al-Buṭayn is its belly, and al-Thurayyā is its tail, and al-Sharṭayn are its horns, and the form of al-Buṭayn is thus 0 0 And as for al-Thurayyā, it is seven stars, six of them visible and one small hidden, by which people test their eyesight; it was named Thurayyā from al-tharwah, and it is the abundance of dew and drizzle, and it has a name from them, al-najm, and although in number it is stars, some of the scholars [said] the intended meaning in His saying تعالى “and the star when it falls” is that it is al-Thurayyā in the majority of sayings, and that the Arabs call al-Thurayyā “mā,” and although it is stars in number, the Messenger of God صلى الله عليه وسلم cast it as a star, so he said: “If the star rises, the ailments are lifted from the fruits and other than them,” and he intended by the star al-Thurayyā, and from them are al-Su‘ūd and al-Muqaddam, some of them said

[p. 30]

If the Pleiades in the evening have appeared, the small-eyed one sees them as seven stars.

It follows the liver of the hot one, and it is as though it were a splint in a knee above a wrist.

And the form of the Pleiades is thus 000 ۞ and the dyed palm of the Pleiades spread out, and it has another that is called al-Jazmā, and it is

below the two al-Sharṭayn. And al-‘Ayyūq is a large red star, and behind it are three stars, among them there is what is called al-A‘lām,

and they are the followers of the lunar mansions. We mentioned it here only because of its nearness to the Pleiades. ۞ And as for al-Dabarān, it is the tail of the lamb, and it was only named

by it, it was said, because it turned its back to the Pleiades. Some of them said: it is five stars in Taurus, called Shāmah, and its form is thus

as though it were the head of a cow, and it follows the Pleiades. ۞ And as for al-Haq‘ah, it is three stars, some of them near to some, and it is the head of al-Jawzā’, as though they were three fingers gathered together, and its form is thus 000 ۞ And it was said: it is the circle that is in the side of the beast from the foot of the rider. ۞ And Ibn ‘Abbās, may Allah be pleased with them both, was asked about one who divorced his wife by the number of the stars of the sky, and he said: it suffices him Haq‘at al-Jawzā’. ۞ And as for al-Han‘ah, and it is five separated stars, and they are two large stars between them three small ones, and the apparent is that they are five, and its form is thus 00000 ۞ And al-Han‘ah was named Han‘ah because every one inclines toward his companion; it is said: ha‘at the thing, if you incline it. ۞ And as for al-Dhirā‘, it is the arm, and it is the arm of the lion, and it is two shining stars. And it was said:

they are two stars between which are small stars, as though they were the claws of the lion, and between them in the sight of the eye is the measure of a whip, and they are two arms, one of them spread out and the other clenched; it is not upon the alignment of the arm, and the spread one is higher than al-Simāk.

The larger of them is called al-Shi‘rā al-Ghamīsā’, because it was unable to cross the Milky Way and its companion followed it, and it wept until

it became dim-eyed. And it was said: it wept because it did not catch up with Suhayl. ۞ And as for al-Nathrah, between them is a small amount, in it is a smear of whiteness, as though it were pieces of cloud, and it is the nose of the lion. And it was said: they are three stars, and its form is thus 000 ۞ And it is between the mouth of the lion and its nostril, and it is called the station of the lion. ۞ And as for al-Ṭarfah, they are two stars in front of the forehead, and they are the eye of the lion.

And it is four stars, one is a shining star, and it is the southern one; between every two stars, in the sight of the eye, is the measure of a whip. It is called

Izzā’ al-Asad, and it follows al-Ṭarfah, and its form is thus ۞ And as for al-Jabhah, they are two stars like the front of the lion, and it is the place of the hair from its shoulders, and between them in the sight of the eye is the measure of a whip, and its form is thus 000 ۞ And as for al-Simāk, they are

among what were shining: al-A‘zal, and it is from the lunar mansions, and al-Simāk al-Rāmih, and it is not from the lunar mansions, and they are two stars. And it is said that they are the two legs of the lion, and it is said that they are the two shanks of the lion, and the other is the other shank. And with al-Rāmih is a star in front of it, and it is its spear. And al-A‘zal is isolated from the stars; there is not near it a star. And al-A‘zal was named

because it has no spear, and it was named Simāk because it rose high in the sky. And it was said: it is taken from al-ghafr, and it is the hair that is at the end of the tail of the lion, and its form is thus 00 ۞ And as for al-Zubānān, and they are the two claws of the scorpion, they are two shining stars thus 00 ۞ And as for al-Iklīl, it is four stars, its description is thus 0000 And it was said: three, the head of

the scorpion.

The Scorpion, as though the Crown were upon the head of the Scorpion. And as for al-Qalb, it is the Scorpion, and beside it is a shining star, and beside it are two stars.

And this is its form 000. And as for al-Shawlah, they are two separated stars, called the star of the Scorpion, and this is its form 0 0. And some of them said: it is the tail of the Scorpion, taken from al-shawl, and it is elevation, as though it were raised, i.e. elevated, protruding.

And some of them said: it is outside the Milky Way. And it has been said: it is like the timbers that are hung upon the head of the well, in which the pulley and the ropes are placed, and they are called al-Na‘ā’im. ☼ And as for al-Baldah, it is six stars of Sagittarius; the sun descends for it on the shortest day of the year. And some of them said: al-Baldah is the opening between the two special ones, and its form is thus 0 0. ☼ And as for al-Dhābiḥ, they are two stars, between which descends the measure of an arm, and in each one is a small star near it, as though it were slaughtering it, so it was named Dhābiḥ.

And this is its form thus. ☼ And as for Sa‘d al-Su‘ūd, they are two stars, one of them shining, and the fourth is below one of them, and its form is thus. ☼ 0000000000. And as for Sa‘d al-Akhbiyah, they are three, as though they were one star.

And this is its form 0 0. ☼ And it was said: they are two stars, and among them are those who said: the first is Sa‘d al-Su‘ūd, then Sa‘d al-Dhābiḥ, then Sa‘d al-Akhbiyah, then Sa‘d Bulā‘, and they are four; the moon descends once in the year. And those which are not other than the lunar mansions are Sa‘d Qā‘irah, and Sa‘d al-Mulk, and Sa‘d Hammām, and Sa‘d Bāri‘, and Sa‘d Naẓīr; so every Sa‘d of these six is two stars, every two stars between which there is in the sight of the eye the measure of an arm, and they are the key of the year. And as for Far‘ al-Dālī al-Muqaddam and al-Mu’akhkhar, each one of them is two stars, between every one and the other five cubits in the sight of the eye, as though they were emptying from the buckets, and al-Fargh is the outlet of the water from the bucket, and from it the two Farghs are named. ☼ And as for al-Rashā, it is a small star which the moon descends; these are the lunar mansions which it traverses in every month, and the moon is in every night in one side of them, between the rising of the sun and its setting fourteen, and between its setting and its rising fourteen, and at the time of dawn it descends in them, and whenever it rises from the east to the west, and the time of their rising is to the left of the prayer-place and the time of their setting is to its right, and that according to the difference of the climates. And the sun also descends these mansions. And know that the Arabs call the mansions al-Anwā’. And it was only named thus because when the setting one falls, the rising one rises. And it was said that al-Naw’ is the falling of a star from the mansions in the west, with the rising of its counterpart from the east opposite it, by an hour in every night except thirteen; and thus every star until the end of the year, except al-Jabhah, for it has fourteen days. ☼ I say: and we have not heard in al-Naw’ that it is the falling except in this place. And the Arabs used to attribute to it the rains, the winds, the cold, and the movement. And ‘Umar ibn al-Khaṭṭāb may Allah be pleased with him said: We were rained upon by such-and-such a naw’.

Section on the rulings of their risings

And that is that the rising of al-Sharṭayn is for the thirteenth of Nīsān, and the sun descends without a crown, and the rising of al-Buṭayn is for the night remaining from it, and the rising of al-Thurayyā is the thirteenth of May, and it is concealed after it falls at sunset for fifty nights, then it appears in the morning from the east. So when it is in the middle of the sky with the setting of the sun, the cold becomes intense and afflictions depart from the fruits, and he, upon him be peace and prayer, said: When the star rises, the afflictions depart. And the rising of al-Dabarān is for the twenty-third of May, and the rising of al-Haq‘ah for eight nights having passed of June, and the rising of al-Han‘ah for the twenty-first of it, and the rising of al-Dhirā‘

Chapter Four — On the Twelve Zodiacal Signs
الفصل الرابع في أحكام البروج الاثني عشر وما لها من الإشارات والارتباطات
On the rulings of the twelve zodiacal signs

for four nights having passed of July, and the rising of al-Nathrah for seventeen nights having passed of it, the rising of the star of al-Shi‘rā al-‘Abūr, and the rising of al-Ṭarfah for the first night of August, and the rising of al-Jabhah for fourteen nights having passed of it, and the rising of al-Zubrah for seven and twenty of it, and the rising of al-Ṣarfah for eight nights having passed of September, and the rising of al-‘Awā’ for nineteen nights having passed of it, and the rising of al-Simāk for two nights remaining of it, and the rising of al-Iklīl for twelve nights having passed of November, and the rising of al-Qalb for twenty-five of it, and the rising of al-Shawlah for eighteen of December, and the rising of al-Na‘ā’im for twenty-one of it, and the rising of al-Baldah for three nights having passed of January, and the rising of al-Dhābiḥ for eleven nights of it, and the rising of Sa‘d al-Su‘ūd for twenty-seven nights having passed of it, and the rising of Sa‘d al-Akhbiyah for eleven nights having passed of February, and the rising of Sa‘d Bulā‘ for twenty-six of it, and the rising of al-Fargh for two nights having passed of March, and the rising of al-Fargh al-Mu’akhkhar for twenty-four having passed of it, and the rising of al-Rashā for four of April, and Allah knows best.

Section on the divisions of these mansions according to the four seasons

Know that for the season of spring are al-Sharṭayn, al-Buṭayn, al-Thurayyā, al-Dabarān, al-Haq‘ah, al-Han‘ah, and al-Dhirā‘; and for the season of summer are al-Nathrah, al-Ṭarfah, al-Jabhah, al-Zubrah, al-Ṣarfah, and al-‘Awā’; and for the season of autumn are al-Ghafr, al-Zubānān, al-Iklīl, al-Qalb, al-Shawlah, al-Na‘ā’im, and al-Baldah; and for the season of winter are Sa‘d al-Su‘ūd, Sa‘d al-Dhābiḥ, Sa‘d al-Akhbiyah, Bulā‘, al-Farghān, al-Mu’akhkhar, and al-Rashā; and for every season seven mansions.

Section on the saj‘ of the Arabs related to the mansions and what was transmitted from the ancients

Some of them said: I read to our shaykh al-Kindī, may Allah تعالى have mercy on him, who said: I read to Abū Manṣūr al-Khawlānī, who said: It has reached me from Abū Muḥammad al-Manāwī that he said: The Arabs say: when al-Sharṭayn rises, the pomegranates become even, the branches become green, the homelands are inhabited, the neighbors exchange gifts, and the poor man spends the night in every place. And when al-Buṭayn rises, the debt is settled. And when al-Thurayyā rises, seek for your shepherd a garment, and when it rises for him tomorrow, seek for him a waterskin. And when al-Dabarān rises, the fires are kindled and the pools dry up. And when al-Haq‘ah rises, the people return from the darkness. And when al-Han‘ah rises, they turn to protection. And when al-Dhirā‘ rises, the sun strips away the rays and drink is kindled in every plain.

And when al-Nathrah rises, the calf becomes weak through abundance and does not obtain in its udder a drop. And when al-Ṭarfah rises, Abū al-Ḍayf becomes easy through lightness.

And when al-‘Awā’ rises, the snakes flee and the air becomes pleasant. And when al-Simāk rises, ownership becomes abundant over wealth. And when al-Farghān and al-Nasr rise, the pools overflow and a bridge flows. And when al-Zubānān rises, every spring is taken. And when al-Iklīl rises, the sayings become invalid.

And when al-Qalb rises, every difficulty becomes easy. And when al-Shawlah rises, the old man’s urination is delayed. And when al-Na‘ā’im rises, blessings are obtained for every standing one. And when al-Baldah rises, then your eating is al-‘aṣīdah, and it is what comes out of butter and ghee from the bottom of the pot.

And when al-Dhābiḥ rises, the equal of the mourner is protected. And when al-Akhbiyah rises, the people love the wearing of cloaks. And when Bulā‘ rises, the earth becomes glitter. And when al-Shawlā [~] rr al-kalā. And when al-Fargh al-Muqaddam rises, then serve and do not regret. And when al-Fargh al-Mu’akhkhar rises, then hasten and do not delay. And when al-Sam

[p. 33]

Know, may God grant you success and you and obedience to Him, that the twelve signs and the twenty-eight mansions, the Exalted said concerning them: And We have certainly placed in the heaven constellations and adorned it for the beholders, and the Exalted said: Blessed is He who placed in the heaven constellations, the verse, and the Exalted said: Possessor of the constellations, and the Exalted said: We determined it as mansions, and al-burj is one of al-buruj, and al-burj is the castle, and perhaps al-burj is called a fortress, the Exalted said: And if you were in lofty towers, and some of the scholars said concerning His saying, the Exalted: Blessed is He who placed in the heaven constellations, they are the place of the seven moving stars, and they are twelve constellations, the first of them al-Hamal and al-Thawr and al-Jawza’ and al-Saratan and al-Asad and al-Sunbulah and al-Mizan and al-‘Aqrab and al-Qaws and al-Jadi and al-Dalu and al-Hut * so al-Hamal and al-‘Aqrab are the house of Mars, and al-Thawr is the house of Venus, and al-Jawza’ and al-Sunbulah are the house of Mercury, and al-Saratan is the house of the Moon, and al-Asad is the house of the Sun, and al-Hut is the house of Jupiter, and al-Jadi and al-Dalu are the house of Saturn, so these are the constellations divided upon the four natures, so each one of them is three constellations and they are called the triangles, so al-Hamal and al-Asad and al-Qaws are a fiery triangle, and al-Thawr and al-Sunbulah and al-Jadi are an earthy triangle, and al-Jawza’ and al-Mizan and al-Dalu are an airy triangle, and al-Saratan and al-‘Aqrab and al-Hut are a watery triangle, and this is a table of the lunar mansions and the constellations and the Roman months, and this is its form:

And the people of interpretation differed concerning the meaning of al-buruj, so some of them said: they are the palaces in the heaven, and its proof is His saying: And if you were in lofty towers, and it was said: they are the stars, and it was said: they are the lamps, and it was said: the gates of the heaven which are called the Milky Way * I say: Ibn ‘Abbas, may God be pleased with them both, has explicitly stated that they are the known constellations to which we have referred, twelve constellations, and that God تعالى divided them into quadrants and triads, and they are divided upon the seven planets as we mentioned 32, and their division upon the form is five planets and its form indicates its front toward the west and its rear toward the east, and it is al-Thawr, and they are three and al-Thawr, its stars, and what comes out of al-Thawr is eleven stars, and they are upon the form of the description advanced from al-Thawr, and its two horns have been struck, and it has been cut in two halves for its course, its front toward the east and its rear toward the west, and among its stars are al-Thurayya and al-Dabaran, and they are the lunar mansions * and the third is al-Jawza’ and is known as the Twins, eighteen stars, what comes out of the form is seven stars, and its form is the form of two standing twins, one of them has placed his hand upon the shoulder of the other and his head and the rest of his stars are in the north and the legs on the edge of the east and their feet toward the west * and the fourth is al-Saratan and it is six stars and what comes out of its form is four, and it is upon the form of the crab, its front toward the side of the east and its rear toward the west and the south in the track of the twins as though they were two lambs for the form of eight and its form is standing and among its stars is al-Asad, bright stars * the fifth is al-Sunbulah and is known as the Virgin and it is twenty-six

[p. 34]

Nūliyā and what comes out of the form is seven stars; its form is a maiden with two wings, she has let down her head upon al-Sarfah, and it is a bright star, and among its stars is al-Simak al-A‘zal, a bright star. The sixth, al-Mizan, eight stars, and its form is standing, and what comes out of the form is nine ۞ the seventh, al-‘Aqrab, and it is twenty-one stars, and what comes out of the form is three, and its form is standing, and among its stars is Qalb al-‘Aqrab, and it is a bright star ۞ the eighth, al-Qaws, and it is called al-Rami, and it is thirty-one stars behind the stars of al-Qurb, and its form is the form of an animal composed of a human and a horse, as though it has confined itself to the neck, not ceasing from the nape of the neck, the description of a man who has placed the arrow in his bow and is bent by the saddle ۞ the tenth, al-Jadi, and it is twenty-eight stars, and it is upon the form of the front half of al-Jadi and the second rear fish to its tail ۞ the eleventh, al-Dali, and is known as al-Dalu, and its mother is two and forty stars, and what comes out of the form is three stars, and its form is a man standing, his hands, in one of them a bucket, and he has turned it over and poured water to the front of his feet and let the water descend from beneath it to the south, and it is also called al-Dalu ۞ the twelfth, al-Hut, and they are thirty-four stars, what comes out of the form is four stars, and its form is the form of two fish, the tail of one of them having been joined to the other. The total of these is three hundred and forty-three. And al-Hamal is the first of the signs, and al-Thawr is a sign in the heaven, and al-Jawza’ it is said that it crosses in the heaven to its middle, and jawza’ of everything is its middle, and al-Saratan is a sign in the heaven, and al-Asad and al-Sunbulah were not mentioned as a sign in the heaven, and some did not mention al-Mizan and al-‘Aqrab as a sign in the heaven, and likewise al-Qaws and al-Jadi and al-Dali and al-Hut, and he said that they are signs in the heaven, and al-Jadi is a star beside the heart by which the qiblah is known, and God knows best ۞ Chapter concerning what each sign has of lands ۞ Know that for al-Hamal are Babylon and Persia and Azerbaijan, and for al-Thawr are Hamadan and the Kurds ۞ and for al-Jawza’ are Hājarjān and Gilan and Sufan ۞ and for al-Saratan is the land of China and eastern Khurasan ۞ and for al-Asad are the Turks and the Tatars and what is adjacent to them ۞ and for al-Mizan is the land of the Romans to America and the Copts of Egypt and Abyssinia ۞ and for al-‘Aqrab are the Hijaz and Yemen and what is adjacent to them, and al-Waqd is Baghdad to Isfahan ۞ and for al-Jadi are Kerman and Oman and Bahrain and India ۞ and for al-Dali is Kufa to the land of the Hijaz ۞ and for al-Hut are Tabaristan and Bahrain and Mosul and Iskandarunah, and this is the inhabited part of the whole earth, and we have mentioned a portion of the regions and what is in them in full and complete detail upon the signs, and God knows best ۞ Chapter on the division of time, and it is four divisions ۞ the first, spring, and it is with some people summer, and it was only named spring so that the spring may be made easy in it, and some named it autumn because the rain passed through it, and its entry is at the sun’s arrival at the head of Libra ۞ and winter, and its entry is at the sun’s arrival at the head of Capricorn ۞ and summer, and its entry is at the sun’s arrival at the head of Aries, and it is with them spring, then autumn, and its entry is at the sun’s arrival at the sign of Cancer, and it is with them summer ۞ Chapter on the winds and what is said concerning them ۞ so the first of them is the north wind, and it is that which blows from the side of the pole; the second is al-Saba, and it is from the rising of the sun when night and day are equal, and beneath it is al-Dabur, and the Arabs claim that al-Dabur returns the clouds and sets them down in the air, then drives them, and when al-Saba is uncovered it meets it, so it places some of it upon some until it becomes one dense mass, and the south wind joins its rear to it and extends it, and the north wind tears it apart; the third is the south wind, and it is that which faces the north, and al-Dabur, which al-Saba faces, and God knows best ۞ Chapter concerning what is between each heaven and its heaven and what has come regarding that of reports ۞ and

[p. 35]

And we have mentioned the doctrine of the ancients concerning the form of the spheres and what is connected to them. As for the doctrine of the legal scholars, and they are heavens with them, and there were narrated in the hadith reports from Ibn ‘Abbas, and it was said [from] al-‘Abbas ibn ‘Abd al-Muttalib: he said, We were with the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, at al-Batha’, and a cloud passed by us, and the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, said, Do you know what this is? We said, the cloud. He said, and al-muzn? We said, al-muzn. He said, and al-‘anan? We said, al-‘anan, and we were silent. Then the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, said, Do you know how much is between the heaven and the earth? We said, God and His Messenger know best. He said, Between them is the journey of five hundred years, and the thickness of each heaven, that is, its thickness, is five hundred, and above the seventh heaven is a sea, between its top and its bottom as between the heaven and the earth, then above them is a world, and He has called them, like the son of the heaven and the earth, and God تعالى is above that, and nothing of the deeds of the children of Adam is hidden from Him, and the proof for it is His saying تعالى: God is He who created seven heavens and of the earth the like of them, so the distance of the whole becomes the journey of these children of Adam, and as for the angel, he traverses the whole in one hour, and likewise the devil reaches the earth before the angel in the heaven. And it was narrated that Abu Razin al-‘Uqayli, may God تعالى be pleased with him, was asked how much is between the heaven and the earth, and he said, An answered supplication. And it was said to him, how much is between the east and the west? He said, The journey of a day. And al-Tha‘labi mentioned from Ibn ‘Abbas: he said, The sun rises every year upon three hundred and sixty windows, and the uppermost window does not rise that day until the coming year. I say: and in the sun are benefits and indications, and among the indications is that it is one and its light shines upon all the horizons and all the world; likewise the Creator, glory and exaltation be to Him, is one and He manages the world. Then the second: that the benefits of the sun are distant, its reaching is near, and God تعالى is distant from creation in essence, near in response. The third: that its light is not withheld from anyone, and for that the provision of God is not withheld from any of creation. And the fourth: that its eclipse is a proof of the existence of the Resurrection and other than it of what indicates its darkness. The fifth: the cloud covers it, and for that al-ghashiyah covered the knowledge. And as for its benefits, they are very many. The first: that it is the lamp of the world. The Exalted said: And We made the sun a lamp. The second: that it is the ripening for their foods and ripening for their fruits, and without burden. The third: that it travels from the east to the west for their welfare. The fourth: that it does not stop in one place lest it harm creation. The fifth: that it is in winter in the lowest of the signs and in summer in their highest for the benefits of the world. The sixth: that it does not gather with the moon in its authority lest each one of them nullify the light of the other. If you say: it is in the fourth sphere, and the heavens did not veil it, yet the clouds veil it, I say: the heavens are subtle, transparent substances, and the cloud is dense because it rises from the earth, and God knows best.

Chapter on the moon and its similitudes and what is in it

It was narrated from Abu Hurayrah that he said: The Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, said, Do you see the full moon and there is no cloud beneath it? They said, Yes. He said, Then do you dispute concerning the sun, there is no cloud beneath it? They said, Yes. He, may God bless him and grant him peace, said, Then indeed you see it likewise. If you say, Why was the example not struck with the sun, since it is brighter and more complete in light, and the moon, its light is from it? I say: for two matters. The first: that the light of the sun overpowers the eyes, so one is not able to look at it. And the second: that it was broken for the sake of the Truth, so God تعالى mended it. And that was when Gabriel struck the light of the moon with his wing, its heart was broken because it was resembling the light of the sun, so God تعالى mended it with two things: one of them is that He made the eyes look at it in the world every month, and the second is that He commanded His Prophet Muhammad, may God bless him and grant him peace, to strike by it the example in the greatest things and the highest of them. If you say, God تعالى said: The eyes do not perceive Him, while He perceives the eyes, I say: we do not claim that the eyes perceive Him in the sense of encompassing Him, but rather the perceived is a ray

[p. 36]

[p. 37]

ten forms in the path of the sun, and they are the twelve signs of the zodiac; among them are three hundred and sixty stars in one and twenty forms that are inclined from the path of the sun toward the north side, among them the Great Bear and the Small Bear and the Dragon and others; and among them are three hundred and sixteen stars in fifteen forms inclined from the path of the sun, and what we have named among the stars the generality of the masters of the craft have never divided them, and Abu Muhammad Abd al-Jabbar known in his book called in Basra concerning the fixed stars mentioned them and said: the stars that are in the northern form, among them the Small Bear, and it is in the form of a standing bear extending its hands, its stars are seven and the Arabs call it the Lesser Banāt Naʿsh, and four stars and the Arabs call it the bier in the form of a square and the three upon its tail, and what is outside the form of them is eight, among them seven stars the Arabs divided [as] Banāt Naʿsh al-Sughrā, the observed stars toward the north pole. I say: and among them is the Great Bear and its stars are twenty-seven stars, and what is outside the form of them is eight stars, among them seven stars the Arabs call Banāt Naʿsh, four upon its body and three upon its tail, and the Arabs call them al-suhā, and among them is the Dragon, and it is thirty-one stars, and its form is the form of a great serpent of many coils, and they began it from four stars in the form of an inclined square upon its head the mark of the scorpion, the benefits, and the Dragon is placed in the heaven, a kind of serpents, and it is the greatest of them; and among them is al-fakka called the Northern Crown, and there is known in the heaven a kind of serpents, some of them the dwellers because of its roundness, eight; and as for al-fakka also, it is stars round behind al-simāk al-rāmih; and among them is the one kneeling upon his knees, its form is twenty-seven stars; and among them are the seven and it is said to it al-lūz and al-subḥ al-rūmī; and among them is the tortoise and its stars are ten, among them stars they draw as al-nasr al-wāqiʿ, and the hen, and it is nineteen stars, and what is outside the form are two stars, the larger of whose stars are in the Milky Way; and among them is the triangle, four stars between the star of the fish and the eagle that is upon the head of al-ghūl; so the total of these northern forms is three hundred and sixty stars. As for the southern forms, they are three hundred and twelve stars, among them Cetus, twenty-two stars, and its form is the form of an animal bare and two legs and a tail like the tail of the whale; and among them is Orion, thirty-eight stars, and its form is a man running and his hand a staff and in his middle a belt and a sword, and among his stars is al-jawzāʾ, a red star; and among them are twelve stars gathered under the leg of Orion, resembling a rabbit whose face is toward the west and its rear toward the east; and among them is the Great Dog, eight and twenty stars, and what is outside the form is eleven behind the stars of al-jawzāʾ, among them al-sunbulah from the stars of al-shiʿrā al-ʿabūr and it is called al-abbān al-marzbān; and among them is al-shiʿrā al-ghumaysāʾ, and they are the stars that rise after al-jawzāʾ; as for Cancer, the forms that are in al-jawzāʾ and al-shiʿrā al-ghumaysāʾ that are in the arm, and the Arabs claim that they are the two sisters of Suhayl; and among them is the Southern Crown, and it is thirteen stars, and what is outside the form is six stars, and its form is a great fish, their stars upon the south of the star al-dālī, its head toward the east and its tail toward the west, and among them is the Milky Way upon the south of ḥazzāt al-ʿa [~], so this is the total of the southern and northern stars. I say:

and this which some of them mentioned is restricted to the famous stars, and as for the غير famous, they are very many, so mention of them will come later, if God تعالى wills.

[p. 38]

{ Chapter on their bodies and what is connected to them } Know that the body of the sun is the measure of the world one hundred and a half, and the body of the moon is the measure of the world thirty-nine times, and likewise Venus and Mercury and Mars, and the body of Jupiter is the measure of the world eighty-two times, and the body of Saturn is the measure of the world ninety-nine times, and some of our scholars said that the body of the sun is fifteen degrees in front of it and likewise behind it, and the body of the moon is twelve degrees in front of it and likewise behind it, and the body of Mars is eight degrees behind it and likewise in front of it, and the body of Venus seven degrees in front of it and likewise behind it, and the body of Mercury likewise, and God Most High knows.

{ Chapter that every star in the heaven is the measure of the world many times } And as for the fixed stars, and they are fifteen stars, al-shiʿrā al-ʿabūr and al-simāk and al-nasr al-ṭāʾir and the heart of the lion and the like of them, every star among them is the measure of the world sixty-four times.

{ Chapter on the traversing of the seven stars the spheres } Know that the moon traverses the sphere in twenty-nine days and a third of a day, and Mercury traverses it in twenty-eight days, and Venus traverses it in two hundred and twenty-four days and a quarter of a day, and the sun traverses it in three hundred and sixty-five days and a quarter of a day, and Mars traverses it in six hundred and thirty, and Jupiter traverses it in eleven years, and Saturn traverses it in twenty-nine years, and God knows.

{ Chapter on the stations of the signs } Know that the station of the moon in every sign is two days and three nights, and the station of Mercury in every sign is fifteen days, and the station of Venus in every sign is twenty-five days, and the station of Jupiter in every sign is a year, and the station of Saturn in every sign is thirty months.

{ Chapter on the dignity of the star } Know that the dignity of the moon is Taurus, and the dignity of Mercury is Virgo, and the dignity of Venus is Pisces, and the dignity of the sun is Aries, and the dignity of Mars is Capricorn, and the dignity of Jupiter is Cancer, and Saturn is Libra, and the Pleiades it was said are the lamp of the world in the heaven because they are the gathering of the stars like the knot, and it is in the heaven, and the Arabs call it the stars because there is not in the heaven a spot more starry than it.

{ Chapter on what belongs to each star of the seven days } Sunday is for the sun, and Monday for the moon, and Tuesday for Mars, and Wednesday for Mercury, and Thursday for Jupiter, and Friday for Venus, and Saturday for Saturn.

{ Chapter on the conjunction of the stars } And the meaning of conjunction is that the star be in a sign and the other in its opposite, and of meeting is that they meet in one sign, so it affects by the command of God Most High every act; that in the Qurʾan, so understand that. If Saturn approaches Jupiter, wars become general in the regions, and a king from the kings of the world and the earth dies; and if Mars approaches Saturn, that is; and if Saturn approaches the sun, that is; and if Saturn conjoins Venus, it indicates the highness of prices and drought; and if Saturn conjoins Mercury, it indicates the rectification of the state of the book; and if the moon conjoins, it indicates the ظهور of injustice in judgments; and if Jupiter conjoins Mars, in the world are many hardships.

{ Chapter }

— Chapter Five —
Chapter Five — Secrets of the Basmalah
الفصل الخامس في أسرار البسملة وما لها من الخواص والبركات الخفيات
On the secrets of the basmalah and its hidden properties

[p. 39]

Chapter on the natures of the planets) Know that the moon is cold, feminine, phlegmatic; in it is an accidental heat because its light

is from the light of the sun, and its dominion is laughter and adornment, and it is yellow bile. Mercury is masculine and feminine, and it is sometimes fortunate and sometimes unfortunate, and the nature of heat is equal in it, and its dominion is speech and writing. Venus is female and she is fortunate, cold, moist; she has phlegm, and her dominion is marriage and joy, and she has desire, and the arranging of the crown, and affectation, and the bringing together of hearts and women and beauty and amusement and laughter. The sun is masculine, hot, dry; it has yellow bile, and it is fortunate by aspect, unfortunate by opposition; its essence is gold, and its dominion is over the sciences, and it has the highest nobility and joy and gladness and kingship. And Mars is feminine, hot, dry; it has yellow bile, and its essence is iron, and its taste is bitter, and its dominion is over the head and the stomach and killing and women and others. Jupiter is masculine, moderate, spiritual, fortunate; it has blood and its essence is tin, and its taste and its color are white, and its dominion is over the wind dwelling in the heart, and it has abundant gifts and leadership. Saturn is masculine, cold, dry, dark; it has black bile, and its essence is lead, and its taste is bitter, and its color is black, and its dominion is over deliberation, and it has heat and injustice and subjugation and coercion. I say: and a people claimed that these zodiacal signs and spheres and moving planets act in the world by influence, and that they are the governors of the worlds, and they argued for that by His saying, تعالى, “and the directors of an affair” and the like * and we do not, for it has been related that the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, when he ascended to the heaven informed us of the zodiacal signs and the stars and others, so whatever has come from him in this الباب is accepted, and whatever is forbidden from it is not to be regarded; rather, the proofs and evidences have been established that the Creator, تعالى, invented them and brought them into being. As for His saying, تعالى, “and the directors of an affair,” Ibn Abbas said that the angels, some of them are entrusted with provisions, and some of them are entrusted with rain, and some of them are entrusted with the winds, as mentioned in the creation of the angels. And if they were directors, then by the permission of the Creator, جل وعلا, because He is the Powerful, the Knowing, the Wise. So glory be to Him whose power is His power and whose wisdom is His wisdom; surely to Him belongs the خلق and the الأمر. Blessed is God, Lord of the worlds, and God knows best what is right.

The fifth chapter on the secrets of the basmalah and what there is of its hidden properties and blessings

Know, may God grant me and you success in obedience to Him and understanding the secrets of His names, that whoever knows what God تعالى has deposited in Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim of secrets will not be burned by fire and it will not consume him, and whoever writes it and makes it successful will not be burned by fire. And all the scholars have agreed that it is recommended to begin with Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim in every matter of importance, following the Noble Book and what Abu Hurayrah, may God تعالى be pleased with him, narrated from the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, that he said: every matter of importance in which Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim is not begun is cut off, and in a narration it is cut off, and in a narration it is mutilated; and its meaning is little blessing and there is no blessing in it.

And it is narrated that the books sent down from heaven to earth are one hundred and four: the صحف of Sheth sixty, and the صحف of Ibrahim thirty, and the صحف of Moses before the Torah ten, and the Torah, and the Gospel, and the Psalms, and the Furqan. And the meanings of all the books are gathered in the Furqan, and the meanings of the Furqan are in al-Fatiha, and the meanings of al-Fatiha are gathered in the basmalah, and the meanings of the basmalah are gathered in its ba’, and its meaning is that everything that is formed will be what will be. And it is narrated that when Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim was revealed the Throne trembled at its descent, and the treasurers said: no one who recites it will enter the Fire, and it is nineteen letters according to the number of the angels entrusted with the Fire—may God preserve us from it. And it is narrated from Jabir, may God تعالى be pleased with him, that he said: when it was revealed

- ٣ - شمس المعارف - أول )

---

[p. 40]

Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim, the clouds fled to the east, and the winds became still, and the seas raged, and they listened to it by the permission of God, and the devils were pelted from the heaven, and the Lord of Might swore: My name is not named upon the sick of longings, nor upon a thing in which there is no escape for it. And Ibn Mas‘ud, may God تعالى be pleased with him, said: whoever wishes that God save him from the nineteen adulteresses, let him write it, and it is nineteen letters; every letter is salvation from every one of them. And whoever increases in its remembrance is provided with awe among the upper world and the lower world, and by it the kingdom of Sulayman ibn Dawud, upon them both السلام, was established. And whoever writes it one hundred times and carries it is provided with awe in the hearts. And it is narrated from Abdullah ibn Umar, may God تعالى be pleased with him, that he said: whoever has a need to God تعالى, let him fast Wednesday and Thursday and Friday; then when it is Friday, let him bathe and go to the congregational mosque and give charity with something; then when he prays the Friday prayer, let him say after it: O God, I ask You by Your name, al-Rahman al-Rahim, God, there is no god except Him, the Living, the Self-Subsisting, to the end of the verse, “to whom the faces are humbled, and the voices are subdued, and the hearts are terrified from His fear”; I ask You to send blessings and peace upon our master Muhammad and upon his family and his companions, and peace, and that You fulfill my need thus and it is thus and thus, so he names it. And he used to say:

do not let your fools know it, lest some of them invoke against some of them, and it be answered for them at the time. And it is narrated from the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, that he said: there is nothing between Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim and between the Greatest Name of God except as between the whiteness of the eye and its blackness. And the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, said: there is nothing between the children of Adam and the devils except Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim; so it is the concealed name which يدلُّ upon that what after it is the Greatest Name, and it is Allah, because the Greatest Name is the Majesty, and it is the pole of the names, and to it they return, and it is among the names like the proper name, because if you ask from al-Rahman, then you say Allah, and likewise the rest of the names are annexed to Him and are known by the Majesty and the loftiness of His exaltedness. And there is for it an additional nobility over the names, and it is that if you remove from it the letter alif, there remains for it “lillah,” and if you remove from it the first lam, there remains for it, and if you remove the second also, there remains “huwa”; so every letter of it is قائم by itself, and it is not like that with other than it among the names, because if you remove a letter from them, its meaning is invalidated. And this name, the Greatest, its letters have not been altered, for it is a nobility over the names and a proof that it is the honored essence, established in might and permanence. And for it there is another nobility indicating the singular essence of lordship and indicating the unification of divinity, for its beginning is the alif, and it is the first of the letters and the first of the numbers, and its end is the letter ha’, and it points to the unification of godhead, and it is not found in other than it among the names, and it says by the basmalah of its state: I am the First and the Last and the Manifest and the Hidden. Then He followed it with the attribute of Rahmaniyyah. He تعالى said:

Say: call upon Allah or call upon al-Rahman; whichever you call upon, so He gave you the choice between saying O Allah or O Rahman, for He is the جامع of the two Rahmaniyyah attributes and the noble speech. So if you wish the seeking of mercy, then say O Rahman, and it is the more specific, because God تعالى is the most specific of the names and the greatest of them in agreement, so it is a رباني name. As for its explanation, it is that He brings things out from nonexistence into existence. And it has meanings other than these, concerning which it is obligatory upon the one looking into them to withhold them from the fools, lest they reach to the doing of the reprehensible and the forbidden acts, and so it fall with God like “he sold,” and when God تعالى intended by it disobedience— we seek refuge in God تعالى from His wrath—O God, do not make us among those who seek help by Your names upon their disobedience. And this name has four letters: alif, lam, and ha’; for it are four natures, and the quarters are four: east and west and north and south; and the angels of glorification are four: Jibril and Mikail and Israfil and Izra’il.

So Jibril is the possessor of the message to the sent ones, and the possessor of victory and subjugation, and by him God تعالى destroyed the disbelievers from the nations . al-qidhah

[p. 41]

and the blowing. And Israfil is the possessor of the Trumpet and the blowing; for him are three blowings: the blowing of terror, as He تعالى said: “then those in the heavens and those on the earth were terrified”; and the blowing of the swoon, as He تعالى said: “then those in the heavens and those on the earth swooned”; and the blowing of resurrection, as He تعالى said: “then he will blow into it another time, and behold, they are standing, looking.” So for every blowing are affairs that are specific to it. And Izra’il, peace be upon him, is entrusted with seizing the souls and their annihilation, and in it is the subduing of the tyrants and the cutting off of the remnant of the disbelievers and the arrogant, and in it is the rest of the believer and his arrival to his Lord and his joy in what God has prepared for him of His generosity and His bounty and His pardon and His forgiveness. And Mikail, peace be upon him, is entrusted with the provisions of the servants and their sustenance and the preservation of their existence, so there is not in the earth a sesame seed except that there is an assistant from the angels entrusted with it for its owner. So for Jibril, peace be upon him, are countless assistants, and he has remembrances and works that differ from them. And these four are kings of days, days being specific to them: for Jibril, peace be upon him, is Monday because it is cold, moist; and for Israfil, peace be upon him, is Thursday, and it is hot, moist; and for Izra’il, peace be upon him, is Saturday because it is cold, moist, its place being earth and death and annihilation. And for Mikail, peace be upon him, is Wednesday, and it is mixed from the four natures. And there are four awfaq specific to them: the sevenfold for Jibril, peace be upon him, and the fourfold for Israfil, peace be upon him, and the threefold for Izra’il, peace be upon him, and the eightfold for Mikail, peace be upon him. And these four awfaq, and this is their description as you see:

the form of the threefold for Izra’il, peace be upon him the form of the fourfold for Israfil, peace be upon him 4 | 9 | 2 3 | 5 | 7 8 | 1 | 6 1 | 12 | 5 | 2 9 | 7 | 6 | 12 5 | 11 | 10 | 8 16 | 3 | 13 | 4 the form of the sevenfold for Jibril, peace be upon him the form of the eightfold for Mikail, peace be upon him 22 | 22 | 61 | 24 | 9 | 26 | 1 20 | 22 | 23 | 74 | 25 | 10 | 21 41 | 4 | 22 | 24 | 36 | 56 | 21 41 | 4 | 23 | 34 | 36 | 56 | 21 26 | 20 | 12 | 28 | 5 | 4 | 7 27 | 14 | 9 | 6 | 2 | 7 | 58 15 | 26 | 7 | 18 | 8 | 9 | 41 27 | 6 | 8 | 5 | 54 | 12 | 1 20 | 20 | 80 | 6 | 7 | 52 | 2 5 | 2 | 35 | 9 | 43 | 3 | 20 11 | 18 | 17 | 8 | 10 | 2 | 8 19 | 12 | 32 | 22 | 9 | 26 | 7 9 | 2 | 9 | 5 | 8 | 21 | 10 8 | 5 | 22 | 52 | 9 | 2 4 | 7 | 16 | 6 | 7 | 4 | 72 | 22 Know, may God grant me and you success in obedience to Him, that these awfaq have a tremendous influence in everything you want; so whoever reflects upon them finds them very correct, by them he does whatever he wishes. And fear God, your Lord, in all your states. So if you desire a work from the works of the four awfaq, then write its seal after counting it and its correctness, and add to it the name of the sought one; you will obtain what you want. As for the sevenfold, it is written on paper or pure white silver on Monday at sunrise, the hour of the moon. If it is for a stone, then write it in the increase of the moon, and if

[p. 42]

If it was in his honor or his fortune safe from the inauspicious, it was more eloquent, and if you desire other than that, from vengeance upon enemies or the darkness of a tyrant, then let the moon be in the waning and the combustion connected with Saturn and Mars, and beware that you do it for other than one deserving it, and pardon is more fitting. He تعالى said: {And that you pardon is nearer to piety}, and He تعالى said: {So whoever pardons and makes reconciliation, his reward is upon Allah}. And fumigate for the good with the good fumigation and for evil with its opposite. So look: if the moon is in an airy sign, suspend it in the air; and if it is in a fiery sign, then in the fire; and if it is in a watery sign, then in the water, or bury it near water; and if you desire sending it, then it will be in a sunlit reed of Faris, and recite upon it what follows; and if it is in earth, bury it in the earth beneath the threshold of his door or your door if you desire to bring him to you; and if it is bone, it will answer you. And this is what you recite upon it: In the name of Allah, the Merciful, the Compassionate. O Allah, I ask You by all Your most beautiful names. Praise be to Allah, which, if placed upon a thing, it becomes humbled and submissive, and if the good is sought by it, it is attained, and if the evil deeds are turned away by them, they are turned away, and by Your perfect words, that if what is on the earth of trees were pens and the sea were replenishing it after it with seven seas, the words of Allah would not be exhausted. Surely Allah is Mighty, Wise. O Sufficient, O Patron, O Compassionate, O Gentle, O Provider, O Loving, O Self-Subsisting, O Knowing, O Vast, O Generous, O Bestower, O Expander, O Possessor of bounty, O Giver, O Enricher, O Merciful, O Compassionate, O Rich, O Reliever, O Tender, O Bestower, O Bountiful, O Good, O Avenger. O Allah, enrich me by Your majesty from Your unlawful, and by Your obedience from Your disobedience, and by Your bounty from other than You, O Most Merciful of the merciful. And I ask You, O Allah, by Your name by which there is no god but He: the Majestic, the Merciful, the Compassionate, the Gentle, the عظیم, the Provider, the Forgiving, the Believer, the Guardian, the Slayer, the Responsive, the Near, the Hearing, the Swift, the Generous, Possessor of Majesty and Honor, Possessor of bounty, the Bestower. And know that the bearer of these names and the one who remembers them, his character is ennobled and he becomes generous with generosity and mercy toward people through them, and he witnesses from the meanings of the Gentle wonders, and acceptance is obtained for him, and his outward and inward are praised, because in it is the Greatest Name of Allah, by which if He is called upon He answers, and if He is asked by it He gives, and it is among the greatest remembrances and the most noble of them. And whoever persists in its remembrance, wondrous secrets are unveiled to him and the sought is made easy for him and the desired is provided in immediate matters. And whoever continues these names at midnight witnesses wonders according to the measure of his aspiration and his persistence, the opening of the hidden secrets, and no one persists in their remembrance except that he sees from the affairs of the world from the higher dominion and every knowledge from the angels is made subservient to him, and they are the perfect words and [~] marvels of secrets. So whoever remembers them with his sufficient name while he desires a matter for himself from where he does not know and it does not occur to his mind and no one mentions it to him, and he is in a weak rank and his aspiration seeks higher than the easier, Allah تعالى makes reaching it without toil. And whoever keeps up the remembrance of the Sufficient and the Gatherer upon something lost to him, he finds it and what he lost returns to him. And its name is التفويح for repelling the painful from the great matters, and the Compassionate is a remembrance for the one who is frightened from wandering; no distressed one but finds tranquility and his terror is calmed. And whoever persists in its remembrance until a state from it overcomes him, with the stomach empty of food, and holds the fire in his hand, he said: it does not harm him, and no breath upon a measure boils; its boiling remains by the permission of Allah تعالى. And he adds to it the Forbearing, the Compassionate, and the Bestower: O Lord, O Bestower. And if these names are written in an hour of the moon and one meets one whose evil he fears, Allah تعالى suffices him against his evil at the time of seeing him. And these three names are not kept up by one whom desire has overcome except that Allah removes that from him. And for the return, that is as I have intended. Among the benefits of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that when it descended, the heavens were shaken by it from the angels, and the Throne trembled at its descent, and with it descended from the angels what none can count except Allah, and the angels increased in faith, and the spheres moved, and the kings were humbled to its greatness, and there were

[p. 43]

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim written upon the forehead of Adam عليه السلام before he was created by five hundred years, and Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim was written upon the wing of Gabriel عليه السلام on the day of his descent upon Abraham عليه السلام. He said: In the name of Allah, O fire, be coolness and peace upon Abraham. And Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim was written upon the staff of Moses عليه السلام, and its writing was in Syriac, and were it not for it the sea would not have split for him. And Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim was upon the tongue of Jesus عليه السلام when he spoke in the cradle, and he used to recite it over the dead and they would live by the permission of Allah تعالى. And Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim was written upon the seal of Solomon عليه السلام. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that it is written at the beginning of every surah of the Mighty Qur'an. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that if a person recites it, the number of its letters, seven hundred and eighty-six times, for seven consecutive days with the intention of a matter, there will be for him all of that from bringing good or دفع evil or marriage of merchandise, for it is married by the permission of Allah تعالى. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that whoever reads it at sleep twenty-one times, Allah تعالى secures him that night from the accursed Satan and from theft and from sudden death, and every affliction is repelled from him. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that if it is read in the face of a tyrant fifty times, Allah تعالى humiliates him and casts its awe into the heart of that tyrant and secures from his evil. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that if it is read at sunrise while you are facing it three hundred times, and الصلاة upon the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم likewise, Allah تعالى provides him from where he does not reckon, and a year does not pass over him until he becomes rich with complete richness. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim for love and affection is that if it is recited over a cup of water, its number 786, and it is given to whomsoever you wish, he will love him with intense love, and if he drinks from that water at sunrise for a period of seven days, his dullness departs and he memorizes everything he hears. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that if it is recited at the falling of rain sixty-one times with the intention of seeking rain, Allah تعالى will complete it. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that if it is recited after the dawn prayer two thousand five hundred times with sincere intention and a humble heart for a period of forty days, Allah تعالى pours upon its reciter from the obscure secrets and he sees in his sleep everything that happens in the world, and that is with the condition of spiritual discipline, for he will see wonders; and let him conceal his secret. And among the properties of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim for the fulfillment of needs and entering upon rulers is that whoever desires that, let him fast Thursday and look upon oil and dates and pray the sunset prayer and recite it one hundred and twenty-one times, then recite it without number until sleep overcomes him. Then when you arise on Friday, pray the dawn prayer and recite it the mentioned number and write it in paper with musk and saffron and rose water and fumigate it with oud and ambergris and its writing the mentioned number. By Allah, besides Whom there is no god, no man or woman carries it except that he becomes in the eyes of people like the moon on the night of the full moon, and he becomes honored, awe-inspiring, eminent, obeyed; and everyone who sees him loves him and fulfills his need and casts his love into the hearts of creation. And this is the description of its writing: Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim, you write it one hundred and twenty-one times, and its writing connected. Another method: and if it is written on gazelle parchment one hundred and twenty-one times with musk and saffron and rose water, and the incense of costus and mastic and frankincense and Java incense, and the one who is afflicted in provision carries it, Allah opens for him and expands his provision; and if a debtor carries it, Allah تعالى pays his debt, and it is for him a security from every disliked thing; and if he writes it in a glass bowl forty times and erases it with Zamzam water or sweet well water and drinks from that

[p. 44]

water, any sick person, Allah تعالى will cure him. And if a woman difficult in childbirth drinks from it, she will give birth immediately. And if it is written on a sheet thirty-five times and hung in the house, no Satan nor jinn will enter it and blessing will increase in it. And if that sheet is hung in a shop, its customers increase and its profit increases and its merchandise increases and it blinds the eyes of the onlookers. And if it is written on the first day of Muharram on a sheet one hundred and seven, a person carrying it will not be afflicted by any harm, nor the people of his house, for the duration of his life. And if it is written 110 times for the woman whose child does not live and the barren woman who has not yet conceived, after her purification from menstruation for three days, and she carries the sheet and the husband has intercourse with her, then she will conceive by the permission of Allah تعالى, and do not lay down the sheet after sixty days, for she will conceive with a righteous son and will not see in bearing him pain nor hardship by the permission of Allah تعالى. And if it is written sixty-one times and carried by one whose children do not live, they will live, and that has been tried and proven, and Allah is powerful over everything. And if it is written on a sheet one hundred and one times and buried in the crop, that crop will be fruitful and protected from all afflictions. And if it is written seventy times and placed with the dead in his shroud, he will be secure from the terror of Munkar and Nakir and it will be for him a light until the Day of Resurrection. And if it is written on a lead tablet and placed in the fisherman's net, his catch will increase. And if it is written once on a card and placed under the bezel of a ring, and that ring is placed in menstrual milk and a bitten person drinks it, and pure it, ah, then the poison comes out by the permission of Allah تعالى. And if the letters are written separated and carried, it will have a great virtue, for the ba is the splendor of Allah, and the sin is His radiance, and the mim is His glory and His dominion, and the alif is His eternality, and the lam is His gentleness, and the ha is His guidance, and the alif is His command, and the lam is the pain of dominion, and the ra is His mercy, and the ha is His wisdom, and the mim is His kingdom, and the nun is His favor. And this and likewise was written from the basmala thus, with b, twenty-one times, and placed in wealth, blessing increased in it. And as a protection, this number was written and to it was added this verse, its letters thus: Peace upon Noah among the worlds, and he erased it and gave it to the bitten one; he was cured at the time by the permission of Allah تعالى. And whoever writes it on a sheet and looks at the letter mim every day forty times while saying, O Allah, Owner of the kingdom, to the words, in Your hand is the good, he does not know from where the good comes to him, and Allah blesses what is before him. And whoever writes al-Rahman al-Rahim fifty times on a sheet and recites over it the basmala one hundred and fifty times and carries it and enters upon a sultan or tyrant, he is secure from his evil and no harm reaches him. And if he reads it every day one thousand times with sincere intention and a humble heart after fasting and spiritual discipline for a period of fourteen days, and in another روایت after every prayer one thousand times the mentioned number, then he sees the spiritual angels and speaks to them and they speak to him and he directs them in whatever he wishes. And whoever writes it broken, cut up thus: م ح ا ل ر ح م ن, also writes the name of whom he desires and the name of his mother, then turns toward him, for he will fulfill his need, O seeker. And whoever writes al-Rahman thus: الرح من seven times and writes with it the name of the slave who flees, and buries it in the house and weighs it down with a stone, and says: O Allah, I ask You by the right of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim and by the right of Your name al-Rahman that You prevent this slave from fleeing, O Lord of the worlds, for he will never flee and will not leave the house in which he is. And whoever writes al-Rahman on a knife for its purposes and recites over it three hundred and thirty-one times and slaughters a rooster with it and separates its head from its body, then when the head swells, take its head after that and bury it beneath the threshold of the door of a house he desires, for all insects will come out of it, and likewise the jinn. And if the rooster's head is fried in good oil and the one with pains is anointed with it, it benefits him well. And if a bleeding woman carries it, it benefits her well. And whoever writes al-Rahim on a banner one hundred and ninety times and carries it and enters the battle of war, no weapon will work upon him and no illness will occur to him. And

[p. 45]

He wrote on a paper twenty-one times and hung it upon the one afflicted with headache; it benefited him. And whoever writes upon seven almonds with a needle of yellow copper on Friday at the hour of Venus, and reads the name its number upon the almonds, and feeds them to whomever he wants, he will love him with intense love. And whoever writes in this manner in a new mirror on Monday at sunrise, and looks much into it, the صاحب اللوقة, may God تعالى heal him. And whoever writes الريحي in this manner in a silver ring weighing two dirhams and carries it with him, God تعالى will provide him with awe and obedience.

And whoever desires to suppress every tyrant, let him write a square of Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim on a piece of lead, and place the name of whom he wants in the square, and fumigate with khentit and red garlic, and bury it near a fire of continuous fuel. And beware that you do not bring the fire near the lead, for the working will perish, and you are the one demanded for it before God تعالى. And this is its form, as you see:

| Bism | Allah | al-Rahman | al-Rahim | Fulan | | Allah | al-Rahman | al-Rahim | Fulan | Bism | | al-Rahman | al-Rahim | Fulan | Bism | Allah | | Fulan | Bism | Allah | al-Rahman | al-Rahim |

And this which you recite over it, you say: Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim, Bism Allah al-Hayy al-Qayyum, to whom the faces are humbled, and the voices are subdued, and the hearts tremble from His awe. I ask You to bless and grant peace upon our master Muhammad and upon his family and his Companions, and to fulfill my need concerning Fulan. O Allah, You know that if he returns from that in which he is, then guide him and grant him success; and if You know that he will not return, then send down upon him Your affliction and Your wrath and destroy him, O Subduer, O All-Subduing, O Able, O All-Powerful, O Allah, seven times, and supplicate with that seven hundred times. For the wrongdoer is either to return from his wrongdoing or perish quickly. So fear God تعالى. And whoever writes the noble basmalah in the middle of a circle eight times, and writes around it His تعالى saying — Muhammad is the Messenger of Allah and those with him to the end of the surah — and fumigates it with a pleasant fragrance at a fortunate time and carries it, then he becomes feared, عظيما, honored among people, and no one sees him except that he loves him and inclines to him by his nature, and all purposes succeed for him by permission of God تعالى. And whoever writes the entire basmalah, and the moon is in Pisces and the ascendant is fortunate, on gazelle paper and carries it on the head, the number falling upon it, then he will live happily and die a martyr, and he will not see in himself, his wealth, or his child what he dislikes. And know — may God grant me and you success in obedience to Him — that Bism is the concealed name, and God تعالى is the Greatest Name, and al-Rahman al-Rahim is an attribute by which He described Himself, so He is the Merciful of the world and the Merciful of the Hereafter. And “Praise be to God, Lord of the worlds” is opposite to “Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim,” so “Bism Allah” is opposite to “Praise be to God,” “al-Rahman al-Rahim” opposite to “Lord of the worlds.” And know that every attribute of His in His saying “Master of the Day of Judgment” is the manifestation of lordship, for He is King and Owner. And upon you is the clarification of the saying and the lights, and for the request of the Day of Judgment, and by the attributive quality of ownership, so He becomes King of kings and manifested to souls by subjugation and overpowering; and by kingship, so He becomes King of kings and manifested to the hearts by ownership, according to His saying تعالى — with a Powerful Sovereign —. And thus all of it is in Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim. So “Bism Allah,” which is the name, to convey all the comprehensive words to the end by the truth, and to raise the call with the tongue of diversion to God, ascent, and “la-hut” al-Rahman al-Rahim descended to the second beginning. Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim is an ascent to the first beginning. In it is the secret of beginning, and in it are the ranks of tawhid, because Bism is opposite to “I bear witness,” and Allah is opposite to “that there is no god but He.” And in it are the ranks of the angels opposite to al-Rahman, and those endowed with knowledge opposite to al-Rahim. And likewise the relation of the quadrangular world, which is His saying al-Rahman تعالى: “Those are the ones upon whom God has bestowed favor, from the prophets, the truthful, the martyrs, and the righteous,” from the Rahimiyyah to the Rahmaniyyah. Therefore the degrees are reached in ascent to Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim. So first

[p. 46]

the circle of Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim. And in the sound hadith: whoever comes and in his scroll is Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim three hundred times, and he was certain of His lordship, God تعالى will free him from the Fire and admit him into the Abode of Decision. And in the Gospel: O Jesus, let Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim be in the opening of your recitation and your prayer, for whoever makes it in the opening of his recitation and his prayer, Munkar and Nakir will not see him. And if he dies upon that, God will make easy for him death and its agonies and the constriction of the grave, and will make spacious for him in his grave as far as the eye reaches, and will bring him out of his grave white of body and his face shining with light, and God will reckon him with an easy reckoning, and his scale will be made heavy, and he will be given the complete light upon the Bridge until he enters Paradise, and he will be called in the arenas of Resurrection with happiness and forgiveness. Jesus عليه السلام said: My Lord, for whom is this and its special property? He said: For you and for whoever follows you and takes by your taking and by your saying; that will be for Muhammad and his community after you. So Jesus عليه السلام informed his companions. And when Jesus عليه السلام was raised to heaven, the disciples perished, and others came who went astray, so they altered and substituted those of the world. Then the sign of faith was lifted from the breasts of the Christians and the monks, and it remained in the breasts of the people of the Gospel until God sent our Prophet Muhammad صلى الله عليه وسلم, so it was written in the beginnings of the surahs and the registers and the heads of letters. And the Lord of Might swore by His might that no believing servant names Him upon anything except that blessing is granted to him therein. And the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم said: Whoever recites Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim while he is a true believer, the mountains glorify for him and seek forgiveness for him, and he does not hear their glorification.
